# Task 0024 — Profile page and account deletion; Pet Portrait and Avatar categories

- **Task id:** 0024 (allocated by `state.mjs`)
- **Operator's brief:** `request-0024.txt` (verbatim), plus the in-session answers (2026-10-27): the auth user is deleted too, except for a Firma Bulucu member (a row in `leadgen.org_members`), whose auth user stays; `welcome_email_keys` (hashes only) always stays; Polar orders, refunds and checkouts are **anonymised** (`user_id` null), never deleted; 10 + 10 new styles, each with its own preview rendered once with OpenAI (about 0.02 USD each). Builds on 0022 (`.mavci/tasks/0022-accounts-wallet.md`) and 0023 (`.mavci/tasks/0023-polar-credits.md`).
- **Supabase:** project `ltjwsmiycijxrqxjsphq` only. The other project of this account is never named in a command.
- **Out of scope:** moving TR to `/tr` and EN to `/` (0025), the domain, a stored language preference, e-mail change, data export, any production deploy.
- **Deploy:** preview only, after verify. Commit, do not push. **The builder writes the migration but never applies it** (G6). No production deploy until the operator says "yayına al".

## 0. Open questions for the operator

Each has a default; the spec is written to the default. Say if you want the other answer before approving.

- **Q1. Amount shown in the purchase history.** The database keeps Polar's *net* amount (tax removed), which for a $3.99 pack reads like "$3.33". Default: **show the pack's sale price from `lib/packs.ts` (`$3.99`, `$9.99`, `$19.99`)**, which is what the buyer paid (prices are tax-inclusive, 0023 Q5), plus the refund state. Alternative: store Polar's `total_amount` from now on (changes the 0023 webhook; rejected for this task).
- **Q2. Where the two new tabs go.** Default: **right after "Çizgi Film"** (`cartoon`, `pet`, `avatar`, `line`, …), so on a phone they are visible without scrolling the strip, and on the home page the showcase shows two samples of each just after the cartoon samples. Alternative: at the end of the strip.
- **Q3. Which photo subjects preview the new styles.** Default: Pet Portrait on the existing approved pet sources `P` (cat and dog), `T` (cat), `D` (dog); Avatar on the single-person portraits `K` (woman), `E` (man), `Y` (older woman), `S` (bearded man). **No new source photo is generated.**
- **Q4. When the auth user cannot be deleted** (a `leadgen` row other than membership still points at it, which blocks the delete). Default: **the Cartoonify records are deleted anyway, the user is signed out and told plainly that the sign-in account could not be removed and to write to us via the contact page; the server logs `account-delete: auth delete failed` with the account id (never the e-mail).** No alert e-mail. Alternative: also send the 0023 operator alert e-mail when Resend is configured.
- **Q5. Deleting while a conversion is running.** Default: **refused** (`409 CONVERSION_IN_PROGRESS`, "try again in a minute") while the account has a reservation younger than 10 minutes, so a running conversion never finds its rows gone.
- **Q6. Language choice on the profile.** Default: two large links, **Türkçe** → `/account`, **English** → `/en/account`; nothing is stored (0025 moves the routes).
- **Q7. OpenAI spend.** Default: **20 renders in 2 batches of 10, estimate 20 × 0.0219 = about 0.44 USD; ceilings 0.40 USD per batch and 0.80 USD for the task** (room for a few `--only` re-renders). Nothing is spent before you approve each batch's plan hash (G3).

## 1. What this task is

**Part A — Profile (`/account`, `/en/account`).** A signed-in user sees, in large type and one column, top to bottom:

| Block | What |
|---|---|
| Krediler | Free and paid credits as two big numbers; **Kredi al** opens the same three pack cards as the workshop (`components/credit-packs.tsx`, unchanged). |
| Satın alma geçmişi | Date, pack (`10 kredi`), price (Q1), refund state (`—`, `Kısmi iade`, `İade edildi`). Empty: one line. |
| Dönüşüm geçmişi | Date, style name, free/paid. **No image** (images were never stored). Last 50. |
| Dil | Türkçe / English (Q6). |
| Çıkış | Signs out, goes to the home page. |
| Hesabımı sil | First press shows a confirmation box saying exactly what is deleted and what stays; only **Evet, hesabımı kalıcı olarak sil** sends the request. |

Signed out, the page shows one sentence and a sign-in link to the workshop. The header shows **Profil / Profile** only when signed in.

**Part B — Two new categories.** `pet` (**Evcil Hayvan Portresi**, 10 styles) and `avatar` (**Avatar**, 10 styles), 120 styles in 14 categories. Each new style has its own preview, rendered once with OpenAI through the existing `scripts/render-jobs.mjs` (data file, plan hash, ceilings, acceptance, `--promote`, exactly as 0019/0020). The existing 100 styles, their prompts, previews and web copies stay **byte-identical**. The workshop's category filter becomes a **single-row tab strip** that scrolls sideways on a phone and never widens the page.

## 2. Decisions

**D1. Account deletion order (brief, answers).** One route, `POST /api/account/delete`, in this order:
1. `getAccountsEnv()` null → `503`. Session → none `401 AUTH_REQUIRED`, Auth unreachable `503`.
2. Body must be JSON exactly `{ "confirm": "DELETE" }` (other keys ignored) → otherwise `400 CONFIRM_REQUIRED`, no database call.
3. `protoolhub.delete_account(user.id)` — **one transaction**: refuse if a reservation younger than 10 minutes exists (`busy`, Q5); mark the user's still-`open` Polar checkouts `refused` / `account_deleted`; set `user_id = null` on the user's `polar_checkouts` and `polar_orders` (anonymise); delete the profile (cascades: wallet, conversions, ledger, welcome grant, sandbox tester row); read `leadgen.org_members` (read only) to answer `keep_auth_user`. It never touches `welcome_email_keys`, so the Q6 hash cannot be lost in any order of failure.
   - `busy` → `409 CONVERSION_IN_PROGRESS`, nothing changed. A database error → `503`, nothing changed (the transaction rolls back).
4. Only when `keep_auth_user` is false: delete the auth user through the **Supabase Auth Admin API** from `lib/supabase/server.ts` (`auth.admin.deleteUser(id)` on the per-call service client; hard delete). Why the API and not SQL: GoTrue owns `auth.*` (sessions, identities, refresh tokens, its own hooks), and the migration may not write to `auth`. Failure (any error, including GoTrue's 500 when a `leadgen` NO ACTION key blocks it) → `auth: 'failed'`, logged (Q4).
5. Always: sign out locally and clear the `sb-` cookies, as `app/api/auth/sign-out/route.ts` does.
6. `200 { ok: true, auth: 'deleted' | 'kept_member' | 'failed' }` (exactly these two keys). `deleted`: profile gone and auth user gone. `kept_member`: Cartoonify records gone, the Firma Bulucu sign-in stays. `failed`: Cartoonify records gone, the sign-in could not be removed (Q4).

Every partial state is consistent: if step 4 fails, the protoolhub side is already complete and a later sign-in starts a fresh, empty profile whose welcome claim answers `email_used` (the hash stayed). If step 3 fails nothing changed. There is no state with a half-deleted protoolhub account.

**D2. Anonymised Polar records (answer 2).** `polar_checkouts.user_id` and `polar_orders.user_id` become nullable and their foreign keys become `on delete set null` (were `cascade` in 0023 — that would have deleted orders with the profile). So deletion through `delete_account` **and** a deletion of the auth user from the Supabase dashboard both leave the orders, keyed by Polar order id, with amount, currency, dates and refund state, and no link to a person. `polar_refunds` has no user column (unchanged).
- `polar_order_paid` gains one guard: a checkout whose `user_id` is null answers `account_deleted` (no credit, checkout refused). Without it the 0023 comparison `p_customer_external_id <> null` is null, and the function would try to credit nobody and fail on the ledger's NOT NULL forever (Polar would retry ten times).
- `polar_reconcile` gains one guard: an order whose `user_id` is null records the refunded amount only — no deduction, no shortfall, no alert (answer 2: "silmeden sonra gelen iade yalnız kaydedilir").
- Both functions are re-created with `create or replace` and their exact 0023 bodies plus the guard (§4). Their signatures, grants and every other line stay as 0023 applied them; criterion 128 diffs them against 0023's block.

**D3. Membership read.** `delete_account` reads `leadgen.org_members` (`select exists (… where m.user_id = p_user_id)`) inside the SECURITY DEFINER function, so the service role needs no grant on `leadgen`. Nothing in `leadgen`, `public` or `auth` is created, altered, granted or written; the migration's only mention of `leadgen` is that one read. The live check (§4) verifies that the function owner can `SELECT` that table.

**D4. The overview.** `protoolhub.account_overview(p_user_id)` (read only, never creates a profile) returns `{ free_credits, paid_credits, purchases, conversions }`: purchases = the user's `polar_orders` joined to their checkout's `pack`, newest first, at most 50, each `{ at, pack, credits, refund }` with `refund` = `none` when `refunded_amount = 0`, `full` when `refunded_amount >= net_amount`, otherwise `partial`; conversions = the user's `succeeded` conversions, newest first, at most 50, each `{ at, style, kind }` (`kind` = `credit_kind`). No order id, no amount in cents, no e-mail. No profile → zeros and two empty lists. No dictionary string may contain the word Cartoonify (criterion 44): the texts say *Karikatür Atölyesi* / *Cartoon Workshop*.

**D5. Routes** (all `force-dynamic`, `runtime = 'nodejs'`, `Cache-Control: no-store`, error bodies `{ ok: false, code, message }` with the Turkish text):
- `GET /api/session` → always `200 { signedIn: boolean }` (exactly this key). No accounts config, no session, or Auth unreachable → `false`. No database call. POST not served.
- `GET /api/account` → config `503`; session `401`; `account_overview` → `200 { ok: true, email, free, paid, purchases, conversions }` (exactly these keys; `email` from the session); database error `503`. The user is always the session's; a `user_id` in the query is ignored. POST not served.
- `POST /api/account/delete` → D1. GET not served.

**D6. Server code.** `lib/supabase/server.ts` gains `accountOverview(userId)`, `deleteAccountData(userId)` → `{ outcome: 'deleted' | 'nothing' | 'busy', keepAuthUser: boolean }` (`nothing` = there was no profile; treated as `deleted`) and `deleteAuthUser(userId)` → `boolean`; it stays the only module besides `lib/env.ts` that handles the service key. `lib/account.ts` (new, pure, no `process.env`, no `next`, no network) holds `ACCOUNT_ERROR_MESSAGES` (`CONFIRM_REQUIRED`, `CONVERSION_IN_PROGRESS`, byte-equal to `tr.errors`), `isDeleteConfirmation(body)` and `refundState(net, refunded)`.

**D7. The profile page.** `app/(tr)/account/page.tsx` and `app/(en)/en/account/page.tsx` render `components/account-page.tsx` (client) with the locale; metadata `robots: { index: false }`; not in the sitemap. `lib/i18n/paths.ts` gains the pair `['/account', '/en/account']`. Selectors (pinned for criterion 131): `main.account-page`, `.account-credits [data-free] [data-paid]` (one element `.account-balance` carrying both attributes), `button.account-buy`, `.account-purchases li` with `data-pack` and `data-refund`, `.account-conversions li` with `data-style` and `data-kind`, `.account-empty`, `a.account-lang[hreflang]` (two), `button.account-signout`, `button.account-delete`, `.account-delete-confirm` containing `button.account-delete-yes` and `button.account-delete-cancel`, `.account-deleted[data-auth]` for the result, `.account-signed-out` with a link to the workshop. Large type: the page's base `font-size` is at least `1.125rem` and the balance numbers at least `2rem`; colours only through `:root` variables (criteria 4–6).

**D8. The header.** `components/site-header.tsx` gains `components/profile-link.tsx` (client) inside `.site-nav`, after the four links: on mount it calls `GET /api/session` once; when `signedIn` it renders `<a class="site-nav-profile" href={localizedPath(locale, '/account')}>{header.profile}</a>`. The built HTML of every page therefore still has exactly the four links (criterion 45 unchanged).

**D9. Categories.** `STYLE_CATEGORIES` becomes `['cartoon', 'pet', 'avatar', 'line', 'drawing', 'paint', 'print', 'paper', 'textile', 'sculpt', 'caricature', 'graphic', 'era', 'surface']` (Q2). Everything that already follows `STYLE_CATEGORIES` (picker groups, filter buttons, `?category=`, landing showcase) picks them up with no new code. `scripts/check-styles.mjs` pins 12; the builder cannot write `scripts/`, so **the orchestrator** changes exactly the two literals `!== 12` → `!== 14` and `expected 12` → `expected 14` (G2; criterion 123 holds the script to HEAD plus exactly that substitution).

**D10. The 20 styles** are §5.2's table: id, category, closing, subject, Turkish and English name and description, and the prompt body. The bodies obey `check-styles` (preserve: whole prompt 260–280 characters). They describe a technique only: no studio, franchise, character or real person; pet bodies keep the animal's own markings; avatar bodies keep the face recognisable and never ask for a younger look or a child.

**D11. The previews (reusing 0019/0020's path).** `data/render-jobs-0024.json` is **byte for byte §5.3's block** (orchestrator, G2). It declares `newStyles` with one variant `A` each, two batches of 10, no sources, no moves, no web copies, ceilings 0.40 / 0.80. `scripts/render-jobs.mjs` is **unchanged**: it already renders a new style's variant on an approved subject, records `usage`, `cost_usd`, `sha256`, `prompt_sha256`, `input_sha256` and the plan hash in `lib/preview-manifest.json`, refuses past the ceilings, and `--promote` refuses until the code equals the choices. Then the existing `scripts/resize-style-previews.mjs` rewrites the web copies (sharp is deterministic: the existing 100 come out byte-identical, criterion 125 proves it).

**D12. The tab strip.** `.style-filter` keeps its markup (`role="group"`, buttons with `data-category` and `aria-pressed`, criterion 57 unchanged) and becomes one row: `flex-wrap: nowrap; overflow-x: auto; max-width: 100%`, buttons `flex: 0 0 auto; white-space: nowrap`. At 390 px and at 1280 px every button sits on one line and the page has no horizontal overflow (criterion 132).

**D13. Legal text.** `kvkk/page.tsx` and `privacy/page.tsx` gain `<h2 id="hesap-silme">`: the user can delete the account from the profile page; deleted: profile, credits, credit records, conversion history, welcome record; the sign-in e-mail is deleted with the sign-in account, except when the same sign-in is used by another ProToolHub product (Firma Bulucu), where only the Cartoonify records are deleted; kept: the hash of the e-mail address (Q6 of 0022, to prevent repeat welcome credits) and the Polar order records without a link to a person (tax and accounting obligations). `REVIEW REQUIRED` stays; a lawyer must still review.

**D14. Reversibility.** The migration adds three functions, re-creates two with one guard each, and relaxes two NOT NULL / foreign keys. Undoing it is by hand (operator only). Account deletion itself is irreversible by design — the confirmation step says so. No ADR beyond this paragraph: the irreversible part is the user's own request.

## 4. The database

The migration, normative (criterion 127 compares the file to this block; 128–130 run it in PGlite after 0022's and 0023's). **Read it before approving (G1): approving the spec approves this SQL.** The two `create or replace` functions are 0023's approved text with exactly the lines from `-- 0024 guard start` to `-- 0024 guard end` added; criterion 128 removes those lines, turns `create or replace` back into `create`, and compares the rest with 0023's block byte for byte. The two `drop constraint` lines name Postgres's default names for 0023's inline foreign keys; the read-only pre-check in G6 confirms them on the live database first, and if a name differed the migration would fail as a whole and change nothing.

```sql migration-0024
-- Task 0024: account deletion, the profile overview, and anonymised Polar records.
-- Everything lives in schema protoolhub. Nothing in public, leadgen or auth is
-- created, altered, granted or written; leadgen.org_members is only READ, inside
-- protoolhub.delete_account, to keep the sign-in of a Firma Bulucu member.
-- welcome_email_keys is never touched: the Q6 hash outlives every account.

-- Polar records outlive the account without a link to it (operator answer 2):
-- the 0023 foreign keys cascaded, which would have deleted the orders.
alter table protoolhub.polar_checkouts alter column user_id drop not null;
alter table protoolhub.polar_checkouts drop constraint polar_checkouts_user_id_fkey;
alter table protoolhub.polar_checkouts add constraint polar_checkouts_user_id_fkey
  foreign key (user_id) references protoolhub.profiles (user_id) on delete set null;
alter table protoolhub.polar_orders alter column user_id drop not null;
alter table protoolhub.polar_orders drop constraint polar_orders_user_id_fkey;
alter table protoolhub.polar_orders add constraint polar_orders_user_id_fkey
  foreign key (user_id) references protoolhub.profiles (user_id) on delete set null;

-- 0023's function with one guard: a refund on an anonymised order is recorded only.
create or replace function protoolhub.polar_reconcile(p_order_id text)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_order protoolhub.polar_orders;
  v_wallet protoolhub.wallets;
  v_total integer;
  v_sum integer;
  v_refunded integer;
  v_target integer;
  v_owed integer;
  v_take integer;
begin
  select * into v_order from protoolhub.polar_orders o where o.order_id = p_order_id;
  if not found then
    return jsonb_build_object('outcome', 'order_unknown', 'deducted_credits', 0, 'shortfall_credits', 0, 'alert_shortfall', 0);
  end if;
  -- Lock order everywhere: wallet first, then the order.
  select * into v_wallet from protoolhub.wallets w where w.user_id = v_order.user_id for update;
  select * into v_order from protoolhub.polar_orders o where o.order_id = p_order_id for update;
  select coalesce(max(r.amount) filter (where r.refund_key = 'order:' || p_order_id and r.succeeded), 0),
         coalesce(sum(r.amount) filter (where r.refund_key <> 'order:' || p_order_id and r.succeeded), 0)
    into v_total, v_sum
    from protoolhub.polar_refunds r
   where r.order_id = p_order_id and r.environment = v_order.environment;
  v_refunded := least(v_order.net_amount, greatest(v_total, v_sum, v_order.refunded_amount));
  -- 0024 guard start
  -- The account was deleted: the refund is recorded on the anonymised order;
  -- there is no wallet to deduct from and nothing becomes a shortfall.
  if v_order.user_id is null then
    if v_refunded > v_order.refunded_amount then
      update protoolhub.polar_orders o
         set refunded_amount = v_refunded, updated_at = now()
       where o.order_id = p_order_id
      returning * into v_order;
    end if;
    return jsonb_build_object('outcome', 'account_deleted',
      'refunded_amount', v_order.refunded_amount,
      'deducted_credits', v_order.deducted_credits,
      'shortfall_credits', v_order.shortfall_credits,
      'alert_shortfall', v_order.shortfall_credits - v_order.alerted_shortfall,
      'free_credits', 0, 'paid_credits', 0);
  end if;
  -- 0024 guard end
  v_target := ((v_order.credits::bigint * v_refunded + v_order.net_amount - 1) / v_order.net_amount)::integer;
  v_owed := v_target - v_order.deducted_credits - v_order.shortfall_credits;
  if v_owed > 0 then
    v_take := least(v_owed, v_wallet.paid_credits);
    if v_take > 0 then
      update protoolhub.wallets w
         set paid_credits = w.paid_credits - v_take, updated_at = now()
       where w.user_id = v_order.user_id
      returning * into v_wallet;
      insert into protoolhub.credit_ledger (user_id, credit_kind, delta, reason, external_ref, free_after, paid_after)
        values (v_order.user_id, 'paid', -v_take, 'adjustment', p_order_id || ':refund:' || v_target,
                v_wallet.free_credits, v_wallet.paid_credits);
    end if;
    update protoolhub.polar_orders o
       set refunded_amount = v_refunded,
           deducted_credits = o.deducted_credits + v_take,
           shortfall_credits = o.shortfall_credits + (v_owed - v_take),
           updated_at = now()
     where o.order_id = p_order_id
    returning * into v_order;
  elsif v_refunded > v_order.refunded_amount then
    update protoolhub.polar_orders o
       set refunded_amount = v_refunded, updated_at = now()
     where o.order_id = p_order_id
    returning * into v_order;
  end if;
  return jsonb_build_object('outcome', 'applied',
    'refunded_amount', v_order.refunded_amount,
    'deducted_credits', v_order.deducted_credits,
    'shortfall_credits', v_order.shortfall_credits,
    'alert_shortfall', v_order.shortfall_credits - v_order.alerted_shortfall,
    'free_credits', v_wallet.free_credits, 'paid_credits', v_wallet.paid_credits);
end;
$$;
-- 0023's function with one guard: an anonymised checkout is never credited.
create or replace function protoolhub.polar_order_paid(p_environment text, p_order_id text, p_checkout_id text,
  p_customer_external_id text, p_product_id text, p_net_amount integer, p_currency text)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_co protoolhub.polar_checkouts;
  v_wallet protoolhub.wallets;
  v_outcome text;
begin
  if p_environment is null or p_environment not in ('sandbox', 'production') then
    raise exception 'protoolhub: unknown environment' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  if p_order_id is null or p_order_id !~ '^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,100}$' then
    raise exception 'protoolhub: malformed order id' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  -- Serialise every delivery of one order, so concurrent retries credit once.
  perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('protoolhub.polar.order.' || p_order_id, 0));
  if exists (select 1 from protoolhub.polar_orders o where o.order_id = p_order_id) then
    return protoolhub.polar_reconcile(p_order_id) || jsonb_build_object('outcome', 'duplicate', 'credits', 0);
  end if;

  if p_checkout_id is null or p_checkout_id !~ '^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,100}$' then
    v_outcome := 'unknown_checkout';
  else
    perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('protoolhub.polar.checkout.' || p_checkout_id, 0));
    select * into v_co from protoolhub.polar_checkouts c where c.checkout_id = p_checkout_id;
    if not found then
      v_outcome := 'unknown_checkout';
    -- 0024 guard start
    -- The account was deleted (anonymised checkout): never credit nobody.
    elsif v_co.user_id is null then
      v_outcome := 'account_deleted';
    -- 0024 guard end
    elsif v_co.environment <> p_environment then
      v_outcome := 'environment_mismatch';
    elsif v_co.status <> 'open' then
      v_outcome := 'checkout_used';
    elsif p_customer_external_id is null or p_customer_external_id <> v_co.user_id::text then
      v_outcome := 'user_mismatch';
    elsif p_product_id is null or p_product_id <> v_co.product_id then
      v_outcome := 'product_mismatch';
    elsif p_net_amount is null or p_net_amount <= 0 then
      v_outcome := 'zero_amount';
    elsif p_currency is null or lower(p_currency) !~ '^[a-z]{3}$' then
      v_outcome := 'bad_currency';
    elsif v_co.environment = 'sandbox'
      and not exists (select 1 from protoolhub.sandbox_testers t where t.user_id = v_co.user_id) then
      v_outcome := 'sandbox_not_allowed';
    else
      v_outcome := 'credited';
    end if;
  end if;

  if v_outcome <> 'credited' then
    if v_outcome not in ('unknown_checkout', 'environment_mismatch', 'checkout_used') then
      update protoolhub.polar_checkouts c
         set status = 'refused', refuse_reason = v_outcome, order_id = p_order_id, updated_at = now()
       where c.checkout_id = v_co.checkout_id and c.status = 'open';
    end if;
    return jsonb_build_object('outcome', v_outcome, 'credits', 0,
      'deducted_credits', 0, 'shortfall_credits', 0, 'alert_shortfall', 0);
  end if;

  select * into v_wallet from protoolhub.wallets w where w.user_id = v_co.user_id for update;
  insert into protoolhub.polar_orders (order_id, checkout_id, user_id, environment, credits, net_amount, currency)
    values (p_order_id, v_co.checkout_id, v_co.user_id, v_co.environment, v_co.credits, p_net_amount, lower(p_currency));
  update protoolhub.wallets w
     set paid_credits = w.paid_credits + v_co.credits, updated_at = now()
   where w.user_id = v_co.user_id
  returning * into v_wallet;
  insert into protoolhub.credit_ledger (user_id, credit_kind, delta, reason, external_ref, free_after, paid_after)
    values (v_co.user_id, 'paid', v_co.credits, 'purchase', p_order_id, v_wallet.free_credits, v_wallet.paid_credits);
  update protoolhub.polar_checkouts c
     set status = 'credited', order_id = p_order_id, updated_at = now()
   where c.checkout_id = v_co.checkout_id;
  -- A refund that arrived before this order is applied now.
  return protoolhub.polar_reconcile(p_order_id) || jsonb_build_object('outcome', 'credited', 'credits', v_co.credits);
end;
$$;
-- Deletes one account's protoolhub records in one transaction. Refuses while a
-- conversion is running. Anonymises the Polar records first, then deletes the
-- profile (wallet, conversions, ledger, welcome grant and tester row cascade).
-- Answers whether the auth user must stay (a Firma Bulucu member).
create function protoolhub.delete_account(p_user_id uuid)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_member boolean;
  v_deleted boolean;
begin
  if p_user_id is null then
    raise exception 'protoolhub: unknown user' using errcode = '22023';
  end if;
  perform pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('protoolhub.account.' || p_user_id::text, 0));
  -- Lock order everywhere: wallet first.
  perform 1 from protoolhub.wallets w where w.user_id = p_user_id for update;
  if exists (select 1 from protoolhub.conversions c
              where c.user_id = p_user_id and c.status = 'reserved'
                and c.created_at >= now() - interval '10 minutes') then
    return jsonb_build_object('outcome', 'busy', 'keep_auth_user', true);
  end if;
  select exists (select 1 from leadgen.org_members m where m.user_id = p_user_id) into v_member;
  update protoolhub.polar_checkouts c
     set status = 'refused', refuse_reason = 'account_deleted', updated_at = now()
   where c.user_id = p_user_id and c.status = 'open';
  update protoolhub.polar_orders o set user_id = null, updated_at = now() where o.user_id = p_user_id;
  update protoolhub.polar_checkouts c set user_id = null, updated_at = now() where c.user_id = p_user_id;
  delete from protoolhub.profiles p where p.user_id = p_user_id;
  v_deleted := found;
  return jsonb_build_object('outcome', case when v_deleted then 'deleted' else 'nothing' end,
    'keep_auth_user', v_member);
end;
$$;

-- The profile page: balances, the last 50 purchases and the last 50 delivered
-- conversions. Read only; never creates a profile. No order id, no amount, no image.
create function protoolhub.account_overview(p_user_id uuid)
returns jsonb
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
declare
  v_wallet protoolhub.wallets;
begin
  select * into v_wallet from protoolhub.wallets w where w.user_id = p_user_id;
  return jsonb_build_object(
    'free_credits', coalesce(v_wallet.free_credits, 0),
    'paid_credits', coalesce(v_wallet.paid_credits, 0),
    'purchases', coalesce((
      select jsonb_agg(x.j order by x.t_at desc, x.k desc) from (
        select o.created_at t_at, o.order_id k,
               jsonb_build_object('at', o.created_at, 'pack', c.pack, 'credits', o.credits,
                 'refund', case when o.refunded_amount = 0 then 'none'
                                when o.refunded_amount >= o.net_amount then 'full'
                                else 'partial' end) j
          from protoolhub.polar_orders o
          join protoolhub.polar_checkouts c on c.checkout_id = o.checkout_id
         where o.user_id = p_user_id
         order by o.created_at desc, o.order_id desc
         limit 50) x), '[]'::jsonb),
    'conversions', coalesce((
      select jsonb_agg(x.j order by x.t_at desc, x.k desc) from (
        select v.created_at t_at, v.id::text k,
               jsonb_build_object('at', v.created_at, 'style', v.style, 'kind', v.credit_kind) j
          from protoolhub.conversions v
         where v.user_id = p_user_id and v.status = 'succeeded'
         order by v.created_at desc, v.id desc
         limit 50) x), '[]'::jsonb));
end;
$$;

-- Browser-held keys get nothing; the service role gets the two new entry points.
-- The two re-created functions keep the privileges 0023 gave them.
revoke all on function protoolhub.delete_account(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
revoke all on function protoolhub.account_overview(uuid) from public, anon, authenticated, service_role;
grant execute on function protoolhub.delete_account(uuid) to service_role;
grant execute on function protoolhub.account_overview(uuid) to service_role;
```

The live check, normative (criterion 130), run read-only after G6 and expected to return **zero rows**. It is 0023's check with the two new functions added to the function lists and four new assertions at the end (Polar user links nullable and `set null`; the function owner can read `leadgen.org_members`; that table has a `uuid` `user_id`; both guards are present):

```sql live-check-0024
-- Task 0024: read-only catalog check of schema protoolhub after all three migrations.
-- Supersedes supabase/checks/0023_live_catalog.sql, which reports 0024's functions
-- as unexpected. Zero rows means every assertion holds; each row names one that does not.
select problem from (
  select 'expected table missing: protoolhub.' || x.n as problem
    from unnest(array['tool_costs', 'welcome_policy', 'profiles', 'wallets', 'conversions', 'credit_ledger', 'welcome_grants', 'welcome_email_keys', 'polar_checkouts', 'polar_orders', 'polar_refunds', 'sandbox_testers']) as x(n)
   where to_regclass('protoolhub.' || x.n) is null
  union all
  select 'unexpected table: protoolhub.' || c.relname
    from pg_class c
   where c.relnamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace and c.relkind in ('r', 'p', 'v', 'm', 'f')
     and c.relname <> all (array['tool_costs', 'welcome_policy', 'profiles', 'wallets', 'conversions', 'credit_ledger', 'welcome_grants', 'welcome_email_keys', 'polar_checkouts', 'polar_orders', 'polar_refunds', 'sandbox_testers'])
  union all
  select 'row level security is off: protoolhub.' || c.relname
    from pg_class c
   where c.relnamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace and c.relkind in ('r', 'p') and not c.relrowsecurity
  union all
  select 'role ' || r.rolname || ' holds a privilege on protoolhub.' || c.relname
    from pg_class c cross join pg_roles r
   where c.relnamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace and c.relkind in ('r', 'p', 'v', 'm', 'S')
     and r.rolname in ('anon', 'authenticated', 'service_role')
     and (case when c.relkind = 'S' then has_sequence_privilege(r.oid, c.oid, 'USAGE,SELECT,UPDATE')
               else has_table_privilege(r.oid, c.oid, 'SELECT,INSERT,UPDATE,DELETE,TRUNCATE,REFERENCES,TRIGGER') end)
  union all
  select 'role ' || r.rolname || ' can use schema protoolhub'
    from pg_roles r
   where r.rolname in ('anon', 'authenticated') and has_schema_privilege(r.oid, 'protoolhub', 'USAGE')
  union all
  select 'service_role cannot use schema protoolhub'
   where not has_schema_privilege('service_role', 'protoolhub', 'USAGE')
  union all
  select 'not SECURITY DEFINER: ' || p.oid::regprocedure::text
    from pg_proc p where p.pronamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace and not p.prosecdef
  union all
  select 'search_path is not empty: ' || p.oid::regprocedure::text
    from pg_proc p where p.pronamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace
     and coalesce(p.proconfig, '{}'::text[]) <> array['search_path=""']
  union all
  select 'role ' || r.rolname || ' can execute ' || p.oid::regprocedure::text
    from pg_proc p cross join pg_roles r
   where p.pronamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace and r.rolname in ('anon', 'authenticated')
     and has_function_privilege(r.oid, p.oid, 'EXECUTE')
  union all
  select 'PUBLIC can execute ' || p.oid::regprocedure::text
    from pg_proc p
   where p.pronamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace
     and (p.proacl is null or exists (select 1 from aclexplode(p.proacl) a where a.grantee = 0 and a.privilege_type = 'EXECUTE'))
  union all
  select 'service_role cannot execute protoolhub.' || x.n
    from unnest(array['claim_welcome(uuid,text,text,text)', 'begin_conversion(uuid,text,text)', 'complete_conversion(uuid)', 'refund_conversion(uuid,text)', 'polar_record_checkout(text,uuid,text,text,text,integer)', 'polar_order_paid(text,text,text,text,text,integer,text)', 'polar_refund(text,text,text,integer,boolean)', 'polar_mark_alerted(text,integer)', 'polar_checkout_status(uuid,text)', 'delete_account(uuid)', 'account_overview(uuid)']) as x(n)
   where to_regprocedure('protoolhub.' || x.n) is null
      or not has_function_privilege('service_role', to_regprocedure('protoolhub.' || x.n), 'EXECUTE')
  union all
  select 'service_role can execute the internal ' || x.n
    from unnest(array['ensure_account(uuid,text)', 'polar_reconcile(text)']) as x(n)
   where to_regprocedure('protoolhub.' || x.n) is null
      or has_function_privilege('service_role', to_regprocedure('protoolhub.' || x.n), 'EXECUTE')
  union all
  select 'unexpected function: ' || p.oid::regprocedure::text
    from pg_proc p
   where p.pronamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace
     and p.proname <> all (array['ensure_account', 'claim_welcome', 'begin_conversion', 'complete_conversion', 'refund_conversion', 'polar_reconcile', 'polar_record_checkout', 'polar_order_paid', 'polar_refund', 'polar_mark_alerted', 'polar_checkout_status', 'delete_account', 'account_overview'])
  union all
  select 'a policy exists on protoolhub.' || pl.tablename
    from pg_policies pl where pl.schemaname = 'protoolhub'
  union all
  select 'a user trigger exists on auth.users: ' || tg.tgname
    from pg_trigger tg where tg.tgrelid = 'auth.users'::regclass and not tg.tgisinternal
  union all
  select 'a user trigger exists on protoolhub.' || tg.tgrelid::regclass::text
    from pg_trigger tg join pg_class c on c.oid = tg.tgrelid
   where c.relnamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace and not tg.tgisinternal
  union all
  select 'tool_costs does not price cartoonify at 1 active credit'
   where not exists (select 1 from protoolhub.tool_costs tc where tc.tool = 'cartoonify' and tc.credits = 1 and tc.active)
  union all
  select 'welcome_policy is not 2 credits, 3 per IP, 1 per device'
   where not exists (select 1 from protoolhub.welcome_policy wp where wp.free_credits = 2 and wp.per_ip_per_day = 3 and wp.per_device_per_day = 1)
  union all
  select 'welcome_email_keys is linked to another table: ' || con.conname
    from pg_constraint con
   where con.contype = 'f' and con.conrelid = to_regclass('protoolhub.welcome_email_keys')
  union all
  select 'credit_ledger.external_ref is not unique'
   where not exists (
     select 1 from pg_index i join pg_attribute a on a.attrelid = i.indrelid and a.attnum = any (i.indkey)
      where i.indrelid = 'protoolhub.credit_ledger'::regclass and i.indisunique and i.indnatts = 1 and a.attname = 'external_ref')
  union all
  select 'wallets lost a non-negative balance check'
   where (select count(*) from pg_constraint con
           where con.conrelid = 'protoolhub.wallets'::regclass and con.contype = 'c'
             and pg_get_constraintdef(con.oid) ~ '(free|paid)_credits >= 0') < 2
  union all
  select 'polar_orders.checkout_id is not unique'
   where not exists (
     select 1 from pg_index i join pg_attribute a on a.attrelid = i.indrelid and a.attnum = any (i.indkey)
      where i.indrelid = 'protoolhub.polar_orders'::regclass and i.indisunique and i.indnatts = 1 and a.attname = 'checkout_id')
  union all
  select 'polar_checkouts does not pin the credits of each pack'
   where not exists (select 1 from pg_constraint con
                      where con.conrelid = 'protoolhub.polar_checkouts'::regclass
                        and con.conname = 'polar_checkouts_pack_credits' and con.contype = 'c')
  union all
  select 'polar_refunds is linked to another table: ' || con.conname
    from pg_constraint con
   where con.contype = 'f' and con.conrelid = to_regclass('protoolhub.polar_refunds')
  union all
  select 'credits were added to a wallet without a matching ledger total: ' || w.user_id::text
    from protoolhub.wallets w
   where w.paid_credits <> coalesce((select sum(l.delta) from protoolhub.credit_ledger l where l.user_id = w.user_id and l.credit_kind = 'paid'), 0)
      or w.free_credits <> coalesce((select sum(l.delta) from protoolhub.credit_ledger l where l.user_id = w.user_id and l.credit_kind = 'free'), 0)
  union all
  select 'a purchase ledger row has no credited order: ' || l.external_ref
    from protoolhub.credit_ledger l
   where l.reason = 'purchase'
     and not exists (select 1 from protoolhub.polar_orders o where o.order_id = l.external_ref)
  union all
  select 'protoolhub.' || x.n || '.user_id is not nullable or not set null on delete'
    from unnest(array['polar_checkouts', 'polar_orders']) as x(n)
   where not exists (
     select 1 from pg_constraint con
      join pg_attribute a on a.attrelid = con.conrelid and a.attnum = any (con.conkey) and a.attname = 'user_id'
      where con.conrelid = to_regclass('protoolhub.' || x.n) and con.contype = 'f'
        and con.confrelid = 'protoolhub.profiles'::regclass and con.confdeltype = 'n' and not a.attnotnull)
  union all
  select 'the owner of protoolhub.delete_account cannot read leadgen.org_members'
   where to_regprocedure('protoolhub.delete_account(uuid)') is not null
     and (to_regclass('leadgen.org_members') is null
          or not has_table_privilege((select p.proowner from pg_proc p where p.oid = to_regprocedure('protoolhub.delete_account(uuid)')),
                                     'leadgen.org_members', 'SELECT'))
  union all
  select 'leadgen.org_members has no user_id uuid column'
   where not exists (select 1 from pg_attribute a where a.attrelid = to_regclass('leadgen.org_members')
                       and a.attname = 'user_id' and a.atttypid = 'uuid'::regtype and not a.attisdropped)
  union all
  select 'polar_order_paid or polar_reconcile lacks the 0024 guard: ' || x.n
    from unnest(array['polar_order_paid(text,text,text,text,text,integer,text)', 'polar_reconcile(text)']) as x(n)
   where to_regprocedure('protoolhub.' || x.n) is null
      or pg_get_functiondef(to_regprocedure('protoolhub.' || x.n)) not like '%0024 guard start%'
) checks;
```

The read-only pre-check for G6 (not a file; pasted into the SQL Editor before applying). Expected: `polar_checkouts_user_id_fkey | c`, `polar_orders_user_id_fkey | c`, `org_members user_id | uuid`, `owner_can_read | true`. Anything else: stop and tell the architect.

```sql precheck-0024
select con.conname as name, con.confdeltype::text as detail from pg_constraint con
 where con.conrelid in ('protoolhub.polar_checkouts'::regclass, 'protoolhub.polar_orders'::regclass)
   and con.contype = 'f' and con.confrelid = 'protoolhub.profiles'::regclass
union all
select 'org_members ' || a.attname, format_type(a.atttypid, a.atttypmod) from pg_attribute a
 where a.attrelid = 'leadgen.org_members'::regclass and a.attname = 'user_id'
union all
select 'owner_can_read', has_table_privilege(current_user, 'leadgen.org_members', 'SELECT')::text;
```

`{email}` is filled by `format`. The page shows the date with `toLocaleDateString(locale === 'en' ? 'en-GB' : 'tr-TR')`, the style with `styleText` (an id that is no longer a style is resolved with `resolveCartoonStyleId`, else shown as the raw id), `kind` with `profile.free` / `profile.paid`, the pack with `format(credits.packCredits, { n })` and the price from `PACKS` (Q1).

## 6. Operator-gated steps

Nothing here is done by an agent unless it says so. Secret values are typed by the operator only.

- **G1. Approve this spec** (`approve-spec`). It approves §4's SQL, §5.2's twenty prompts and §5.3's spending ceilings. Answer Q1–Q7 first if you want anything other than the defaults.
- **G2. Orchestrator, free, before build:** write `data/render-jobs-0024.json` from §5.3; in `scripts/check-styles.mjs` replace exactly `STYLE_CATEGORIES.length !== 12` with `STYLE_CATEGORIES.length !== 14` and `expected 12` with `expected 14` (nothing else); run `--task 0024 --plan 1` and `--plan 2` and show the operator both plans, prompts and **PLAN HASH**es. Expected: 10 renders each, estimated 0.2190 USD each, 0.4380 USD in total.
- **G3. Operator: approve the spending, batch 1 (Pet Portrait), about 0.22 USD.** The orchestrator then runs `node --env-file=.env.local --disable-warning=MODULE_TYPELESS_PACKAGE_JSON scripts/render-jobs.mjs --task 0024 --render 1 --approved-plan <hash>`. The script stops before any call that would pass 0.40 USD for the batch or 0.80 USD for the task. You look at the 10 renders (`assets/trials/0024/`); a weak one may be re-rendered with `--only <id>.A.<K>` (about 0.02 USD each) before you accept with `--accept 1 --result <hash> --choose <id>=A,…` (all ten).
- **G4. Operator: batch 2 (Avatar), about 0.22 USD**, the same way. Then `--finalize` writes `reports/0024-choices.json`.
- **G5. Build, then promote (orchestrator, free).** The builder does step D (the code, from `reports/0024-choices.json` and §5.2). Then `--promote` (refuses until the code equals the choices), then `node --disable-warning=MODULE_TYPELESS_PACKAGE_JSON scripts/resize-style-previews.mjs`, `npm run check`, `npm run build`. Expected: 20 previews promoted, 120 web copies written, the 100 old ones byte-identical.
- **G6. After verify: database (operator).** Backups outside the repo as 0023's G4; run §4's `precheck-0024` read-only and compare with the expected four rows; then apply `supabase/migrations/20261029000000_protoolhub_account.sql` with the Supabase MCP `apply_migration` on `ltjwsmiycijxrqxjsphq` or that project's SQL Editor. **Never `supabase db push`, never `db reset`.** Then run `supabase/checks/0024_live_catalog.sql` read-only: zero rows.
- **G7. Before verify (orchestrator):** copy §7's `operator-steps-0024` block to `docs/0024-operator-steps.md`; `.env.local` holds none of 0022's or 0023's account/payment names; no `next dev`/`next start` of this project running.
- **G8. Preview test (operator):** on a preview, sign in with a throwaway account, convert once, open **Profil**, check credits and history, delete the account, confirm you are signed out and that signing in again with the same address gives no free credits. Do **not** test deletion with your own Firma Bulucu account unless you mean it (it keeps the sign-in but deletes your Cartoonify credits).
- **G9.** Commit, do not push. No production deploy until "yayına al".

**Expected OpenAI cost:** 20 renders × about 0.0219 USD = **about 0.44 USD** (0.80 USD hard ceiling for the task, 0.40 USD per batch). No other paid call in this task.

## 7. The operator document — criterion 137

The orchestrator copies this block to `docs/0024-operator-steps.md` (G7). Plain Turkish.

```md operator-steps-0024
# Görev 0024 — Profil sayfası, hesap silme ve iki yeni kategori: operatör adımları

Bu belgede hiçbir gizli değer yoktur; anahtarları yalnız siz, ilgili ekrana yazarsınız.

## A. Önizleme görselleri (ücretli, sizin onayınızla)

1. Asistan size iki plan gösterir: 1. parti Evcil Hayvan Portresi (10 görsel), 2. parti Avatar (10 görsel). Her birinin tahmini maliyeti yaklaşık 0,22 USD'dir; toplam yaklaşık 0,44 USD. Görevin üst sınırı 0,80 USD, parti başına 0,40 USD'dir; betik bu sınırı aşacak bir çağrıdan önce durur.
2. Her parti için planı ve "PLAN HASH" değerini okuyun, onay verin. Onaysız hiçbir görsel üretilmez.
3. Üretilen görsellere `assets/trials/0024/` klasöründen bakın. Beğenmediğinizi tek tek yeniden ürettirebilirsiniz (her biri yaklaşık 0,02 USD). Beğendiğinizde partiyi kabul edin.
4. Mevcut 100 stilin önizlemeleri değişmez; yalnız 20 yeni görsel eklenir.

## B. Veritabanı (doğrulamadan sonra)

1. **Yedek alın** (0023'teki gibi, depo klasörünün dışına; her komutta `--project-ref ltjwsmiycijxrqxjsphq`).
2. **Ön kontrol:** spec'teki `precheck-0024` sorgusunu SQL Editor'de çalıştırın. Dört satır görmelisiniz: `polar_checkouts_user_id_fkey | c`, `polar_orders_user_id_fkey | c`, `org_members user_id | uuid`, `owner_can_read | true`. Farklıysa durun ve asistana söyleyin.
3. **Migration:** `supabase/migrations/20261029000000_protoolhub_account.sql` dosyasını okuyun, sonra Supabase MCP `apply_migration` ile (proje `ltjwsmiycijxrqxjsphq`) ya da SQL Editor'e yapıştırarak çalıştırın. **Asla `supabase db push`, asla `db reset`.** Migration yalnız `protoolhub` şemasını değiştirir; `leadgen.org_members` tablosunu yalnız okur.
4. **Kontrol:** `supabase/checks/0024_live_catalog.sql` dosyasını çalıştırın: **sıfır satır** dönmeli. (0023'ün kontrolü artık iki yeni işlevi "unexpected" diye gösterir; bu beklenir.)

## C. Hesap silme nasıl çalışır

- Kullanıcı Profil sayfasında "Hesabımı sil" der, ne silineceğini okur ve "Evet, hesabımı kalıcı olarak sil" ile onaylar.
- Silinenler: profil, krediler (ücretli dahil), kredi ve dönüşüm geçmişi, hoş geldin kaydı; ardından giriş hesabı ve e-posta adresi.
- Kalanlar: e-posta adresinin geri çevrilemez özeti (aynı adrese ikinci kez ücretsiz kredi verilmez) ve kişiye bağlı olmayan Polar sipariş/iade kayıtları (sipariş no, tutar, tarih). Silmeden sonra gelen bir iade yalnız kaydedilir; kredi düşülecek hesap yoktur.
- Kişi Firma Bulucu üyesiyse yalnız Cartoonify kayıtları silinir; giriş hesabı kalır.
- Giriş hesabı silinemezse (Firma Bulucu'da kişinin adına başka kayıtlar varsa) Cartoonify kayıtları yine silinir, kişi çıkış yapar ve "iletişim sayfasından bize yazın" mesajını görür. Vercel günlüklerinde `account-delete: auth delete failed` satırını arayın; giriş hesabını Supabase panelinden elle silin ya da kişiye durumu açıklayın. Bu satırda e-posta yoktur, yalnız hesap kimliği vardır.
- Silme sırasında süren bir dönüşüm varsa kişiden bir dakika sonra tekrar denemesi istenir.

## D. Önizlemede deneme

1. Atma bir hesapla giriş yapın, bir dönüşüm yapın.
2. Üst menüde "Profil" görünmeli. Profilde kredileri, satın alma ve dönüşüm geçmişini (görsel yok) görün; "Türkçe / English" bağlantılarını deneyin.
3. Atölyede kategori sekmeleri tek satırda olmalı; telefonda yana kaydırılmalı, sayfa yana taşmamalı. "Evcil Hayvan Portresi" ve "Avatar" sekmelerinde 10'ar stil görünmeli.
4. Hesabı silin: "Hesabınız silindi." görünmeli ve oturum kapanmalı. Aynı adresle tekrar giriş yapınca ücretsiz kredi verilmemeli.
5. Kendi Firma Bulucu hesabınızla silme denemesi yapmayın (Cartoonify kredileriniz silinir).
```

**Discrimination — the wrong implementations these catch:** deleting Polar orders with the account (129: the order row must survive with `user_id` null; 128/130: `set null` required); losing the Q6 hash (129: the hash after deletion, `email_used` on return; 127: the SQL may not name `welcome_email_keys`); deleting the auth user of a Firma Bulucu member (124: no admin call when `keep_auth_user`; 129: membership read); deleting the auth user before the protoolhub records, or by SQL (124: RPC then admin call, in that order; 127: nothing in `auth`); deleting without confirmation (124: five bad bodies; 131: the first click sends nothing); a refund after deletion crashing the webhook or crediting nobody (129); a header link in the static HTML (45, carried); the 100 old previews or prompts touched (125, 126, 22); a wrapping filter or a page that overflows on a phone (132); a profile page that shows images (131: no `img` in the history).

**Not checkable here, done by the operator:** G3–G4's visual acceptance of the twenty renders; G6's pre-check and live check on the real database (including that the real function owner can read `leadgen.org_members`, which PGlite's superuser cannot disprove); G8's real deletion on a preview against the real GoTrue (including the NO ACTION failure path, which only stubs exercise here).

## 9. How this set was proven

- **Lengths:** 112 criteria, every run at most 7 800 characters (longest: criterion 29, carried, 7 791). New criteria 120–138 are a 706-character loader of the harness-0024 block. Renewed: 22 (2 542), 35 (1 898), 39 (1 600), 40 (1 555), 41 (3 552), 59 (2 678), 67 (5 768).
- **Database, both directions, run 2026-10-27** in a scratch copy holding this spec, 0022's and 0023's specs and the three migration files extracted from the blocks (PGlite 0.5.12 from the project's node_modules): **green** for 127, 128, 129 and 130 on §4's SQL. **Red** on six sabotaged copies of the 0024 block: the order.paid guard removed (129: a late order.paid raises on the NOT NULL ledger), orders deleted instead of anonymised (129), the membership read removed (127, 129), orders cascading again (128, 129, 130), the busy check disabled (129), the overview leaking an order id (129). 130 is loud on its 12 variants by construction. Note: removing only the order.paid guard's code but leaving its comment passes 128 (it checks the marker and the diff); 129 catches it.
- **Red on the current tree (run 2026-10-27):** 35, 40, 41, 59, 67 (renewed: 100 styles, 12 categories, no 0024 choices), 120 (no routes, no lib/account.ts), 123 (check-styles unchanged), 125 (no 0024 choices), 126 (no new styles, 12 categories), 127–130 (no migration), 134 (no texts), 135 (no data file), 136 (no sections), 137 (no document). **Green on the current tree, as guards should be:** 22 (renewed freeze) and 138 (scope).
- **Not proven:** 121, 122, 124, 131, 132 (server and browser) and 133 were not run: they need a build with the new routes (red today by construction: 404 instead of the pinned answers), and no reference implementation was built, so their green direction is unproven; the same holds for the green direction of 120, 123, 125, 126, 134–137. 39 (renewed) needs a build and was not run. The pinned interfaces (routes, JSON keys, RPC names and arguments, selectors, texts, file names) are what make them fair; a criterion that fails correct code is a criterion defect, and the builder escalates it rather than bending the code.

## 10. The harness

The new criteria 120–138 are a short loader that extracts this block from **this approved file** and runs one named check, so their logic is approved with the spec and never passes through a shell argument. The shared helpers (PGlite prelude, cookie jar, stub servers, next start) are harness-0023's, copied; the stub additionally answers the Auth admin API (`DELETE /auth/v1/admin/users/<id>`, service key only) and the two new RPCs, and PGlite's `leadgen.org_members` stand-in has the live columns (`org_id`, `user_id`, `role`, `created_at`).

```js harness-0024
// harness-0024: every new and renewed check of task 0024.
// Extracted from the approved spec by the criterion loader and run with the
// repository root as the working directory. No network: Supabase Auth, its admin
// API and PostgREST are local stubs; the database is PGlite. The shared helpers
// are 0023's (harness-0023), copied, with the stub extended for 0024.
import fs from 'node:fs'
import os from 'node:os'
import path from 'node:path'
import http from 'node:http'
import crypto from 'node:crypto'
import { spawn, spawnSync } from 'node:child_process'
import { createRequire } from 'node:module'
import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url'

const req = createRequire(path.join(process.cwd(), 'package.json'))
const LF = '\n'
const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms))
const R = (f) => (fs.existsSync(f) ? fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8') : null)
const SPEC = '.mavci/tasks/0024-profile-categories.md'
const SPEC22 = '.mavci/tasks/0022-accounts-wallet.md'
const MIG22 = 'supabase/migrations/20261027000000_protoolhub_wallet.sql'
const MIG = 'supabase/migrations/20261028000000_protoolhub_polar.sql'
const LIVE = 'supabase/checks/0023_live_catalog.sql'
const DOC = 'docs/0023-operator-steps.md'
const TS = '1x' + '0'.repeat(31) + 'AA'
const ANON = 'anon-stub-key'
const SERVICE = 'service-stub-key'
const PTOKEN = 'polar-stub-token'
const PROD = { p10: 'prod-10-stub', p30: 'prod-30-stub', p75: 'prod-75-stub' }
const ALERT_TO = 'ops-alert@example.test'
const RKEY = 'resend-stub-key'
// The webhook secret is computed, never written out: whsec_ plus base64 of 32 bytes.
const WH = 'whsec_' + crypto.createHash('sha256').update('cartoonify-0023-stub-webhook').digest('base64')
const WH_OTHER = 'whsec_' + crypto.createHash('sha256').update('another-endpoint').digest('base64')
const T22 = ['tool_costs', 'welcome_policy', 'profiles', 'wallets', 'conversions', 'credit_ledger', 'welcome_grants', 'welcome_email_keys']
const TNEW = ['polar_checkouts', 'polar_orders', 'polar_refunds', 'sandbox_testers']
const TABLES = T22.concat(TNEW)
const F22 = ['ensure_account', 'claim_welcome', 'begin_conversion', 'complete_conversion', 'refund_conversion']
const API = ['claim_welcome(uuid,text,text,text)', 'begin_conversion(uuid,text,text)', 'complete_conversion(uuid)', 'refund_conversion(uuid,text)', 'polar_record_checkout(text,uuid,text,text,text,integer)', 'polar_order_paid(text,text,text,text,text,integer,text)', 'polar_refund(text,text,text,integer,boolean)', 'polar_mark_alerted(text,integer)', 'polar_checkout_status(uuid,text)']
const HEX = /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/
const H64 = (s) => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(String(s)).digest('hex')
const UID = (n) => '00000000-0000-4000-8000-' + String(n).padStart(12, '0')
const J = (v) => (typeof v === 'string' ? JSON.parse(v) : v)
const C = {}

export async function run(id) {
  const fn = C[id]
  if (!fn) throw new Error('the harness has no check ' + id)
  const bad = []
  let note = ''
  try { note = (await fn(bad)) || '' } catch (e) { bad.push('threw: ' + (e && e.message)) }
  // Exit only after the write has flushed: on Windows, process.exit() racing a
  // pending pipe write aborts node (libuv UV_HANDLE_CLOSING, exit 127).
  const end = (stream, msg, code) => { process.exitCode = code; stream.write(msg + LF, () => setTimeout(() => process.exit(code), 50)) }
  if (bad.length) return end(process.stderr, 'Error: ' + bad.length + ' problem(s): ' + bad.slice(0, 15).join('; '), 1)
  end(process.stdout, 'ok: ' + id + (note ? ' - ' + note : ''), 0)
}

async function until(fn, ms, what) {
  const end = Date.now() + ms
  for (;;) {
    try { const v = await fn(); if (v) return v } catch (e) {}
    if (Date.now() > end) throw new Error('timed out waiting for ' + what)
    await sleep(150)
  }
}

function block(tag, file) {
  const t = fs.readFileSync(file || SPEC, 'utf8').split('\r\n').join(LF)
  const fence = String.fromCharCode(96).repeat(3)
  const a = t.indexOf(fence + tag + LF)
  if (a < 0) throw new Error('spec block ' + tag + ' not found in ' + (file || SPEC))
  const s = a + fence.length + tag.length + 1
  return t.slice(s, t.indexOf(LF + fence, s) + 1)
}

async function dict(l) { return (await import(pathToFileURL(path.resolve('lib/i18n/' + l + '.ts')).href))[l] }
const fmt = (s, n) => String(s).split('{n}').join(String(n))

const walk = (d) => (fs.existsSync(d) ? fs.readdirSync(d, { recursive: true }).map((x) => d + '/' + String(x).split(String.fromCharCode(92)).join('/')).filter((p) => fs.statSync(p).isFile()) : [])
const stripSql = (s) => s.replace(/--[^\n]*/g, ' ').replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, ' ')

/* ------------------------------------------------------------- PGlite */

const PRELUDE = [
  'create role anon nologin', 'create role authenticated nologin', 'create role service_role nologin bypassrls',
  'create schema auth', 'create table auth.users (id uuid primary key, email text, email_confirmed_at timestamptz)',
  'create schema leadgen', 'create table leadgen.org_members (org_id uuid, user_id uuid references auth.users (id) on delete cascade, role text, created_at timestamptz not null default now())', 'alter table leadgen.org_members enable row level security',
  'grant usage on schema leadgen to authenticated', 'grant select on leadgen.org_members to service_role',
  'create table public.companies (id int primary key)', 'alter table public.companies enable row level security', 'grant select on public.companies to service_role',
].join(';' + LF) + ';'

async function outside(db) {
  const q = async (s) => JSON.stringify((await db.query(s)).rows)
  return [
    await q("select n.nspname, c.relname, c.relkind, c.relacl::text acl, c.relrowsecurity from pg_class c join pg_namespace n on n.oid = c.relnamespace where n.nspname not in ('protoolhub', 'pg_catalog', 'information_schema', 'pg_toast') order by 1, 2"),
    await q("select nspname, nspacl::text from pg_namespace where nspname <> 'protoolhub' order by 1"),
    await q("select tgrelid::regclass::text t, tgname from pg_trigger where not tgisinternal order by 1, 2"),
    await q("select n.nspname, p.proname, p.proacl::text from pg_proc p join pg_namespace n on n.oid = p.pronamespace where n.nspname not in ('protoolhub', 'pg_catalog', 'information_schema') order by 1, 2"),
    await q('select rolname, rolsuper, rolbypassrls from pg_roles order by 1'),
  ].join(LF)
}

// Everything task 0022 created, as the catalog sees it: 0023 must leave it alone.
async function snap22(db) {
  const q = async (s) => JSON.stringify((await db.query(s)).rows)
  const tl = T22.map((t) => "'" + t + "'").join(', ')
  const fl = F22.map((f) => "'" + f + "'").join(', ')
  return [
    await q("select c.relname, c.relacl::text, c.relrowsecurity from pg_class c where c.relnamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace and c.relname in (" + tl + ') order by 1'),
    await q("select c.relname, a.attname, format_type(a.atttypid, a.atttypmod) t, a.attnotnull, pg_get_expr(d.adbin, d.adrelid) def from pg_attribute a join pg_class c on c.oid = a.attrelid left join pg_attrdef d on d.adrelid = a.attrelid and d.adnum = a.attnum where c.relnamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace and c.relname in (" + tl + ') and a.attnum > 0 and not a.attisdropped order by 1, 2'),
    await q("select c.relname, con.conname, pg_get_constraintdef(con.oid) d from pg_constraint con join pg_class c on c.oid = con.conrelid where c.relnamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace and c.relname in (" + tl + ') order by 1, 2'),
    await q("select p.oid::regprocedure::text sig, pg_get_functiondef(p.oid) d, p.proacl::text acl from pg_proc p where p.pronamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace and p.proname in (" + fl + ') order by 1'),
    await q("select nspacl::text from pg_namespace where nspname = 'protoolhub'"),
    await q('select tool, credits, active from protoolhub.tool_costs order by 1'),
    await q('select free_credits, per_ip_per_day, per_device_per_day from protoolhub.welcome_policy'),
  ].join(LF)
}

async function pg(after) {
  const r22 = R(MIG22)
  const raw = R(MIG)
  if (r22 === null) throw new Error(MIG22 + ' is missing')
  if (raw === null) throw new Error(MIG + ' is missing')
  const { PGlite } = req('@electric-sql/pglite')
  const db = new PGlite()
  await db.exec(PRELUDE)
  const before = await outside(db)
  await db.exec(r22)
  const mid = await snap22(db)
  await db.exec(raw)
  if (after) await db.exec(after)
  return { db, before, mid }
}

const one = async (db, sql, params) => (await db.query(sql, params || [])).rows[0]
const call = async (db, fn, args) => J((await one(db, 'select protoolhub.' + fn + '(' + args.map((_, i) => '$' + (i + 1)).join(', ') + ') as r', args)).r)
async function as(db, role, fn) { await db.exec('set role ' + role); try { return await fn() } finally { await db.exec('reset role') } }
async function denied(db, role, sql) { try { await as(db, role, () => db.query(sql)); return false } catch (e) { return true } }
async function throws(fn) { try { await fn(); return false } catch (e) { return true } }
async function users(db, list) { for (const [n, email] of list) await db.query('insert into auth.users (id, email, email_confirmed_at) values ($1, $2, now())', [UID(n), email]) }
const S = (db, fn, args) => as(db, 'service_role', () => call(db, fn, args))
const paidOf = async (db, n) => (await one(db, 'select paid_credits p, free_credits f from protoolhub.wallets where user_id = $1', [UID(n)])) || { p: null, f: null }

async function ledgerOk(db, bad, n) {
  const w = await one(db, 'select free_credits f, paid_credits p from protoolhub.wallets where user_id = $1', [UID(n)])
  const l = (await db.query('select credit_kind k, delta d, free_after fa, paid_after pa from protoolhub.credit_ledger where user_id = $1 order by id', [UID(n)])).rows
  let f = 0
  let p = 0
  for (const r of l) {
    if (r.k === 'free') f += r.d; else p += r.d
    if (r.fa !== f || r.pa !== p) { bad.push('user ' + n + ': a ledger row says ' + r.fa + '/' + r.pa + ', the running sum is ' + f + '/' + p); break }
  }
  if (!w || w.f !== f || w.p !== p) bad.push('user ' + n + ': wallet ' + JSON.stringify(w) + ' differs from the ledger ' + f + '/' + p)
}

/* ------------------------------------------------- stubs and next start */

class Jar {
  constructor() { this.c = new Map() }
  take(res) {
    for (const line of res.headers.getSetCookie()) {
      const parts = line.split(';')
      const i = parts[0].indexOf('=')
      const name = parts[0].slice(0, i).trim()
      const value = parts[0].slice(i + 1).trim()
      const attrs = parts.slice(1).map((a) => a.trim().toLowerCase())
      const gone = value === '' || attrs.some((a) => a === 'max-age=0' || (a.indexOf('expires=') === 0 && Date.parse(a.slice(8)) < Date.now()))
      if (gone) this.c.delete(name); else this.c.set(name, value)
    }
    return res
  }
  header() { return [...this.c].map(([k, v]) => k + '=' + v).join('; ') }
}

async function stub() {
  const St = { overview: null, del: null, adminFail: false, userDown: false, calls: [], rpcFail: null, polarDown: false, resendDown: false, record: 'recorded', paid: null, alert: 0, status: null, otps: [], users: {}, n: 0 }
  const user = (email) => {
    if (!St.users[email]) { const n = Object.keys(St.users).length + 1; St.users[email] = { id: UID(100 + n), aud: 'authenticated', role: 'authenticated', email, email_confirmed_at: '2026-10-27T00:00:00Z', app_metadata: { provider: 'email' }, user_metadata: {}, created_at: '2026-10-27T00:00:00Z' } }
    return St.users[email]
  }
  const server = http.createServer((q, s) => {
    const ch = []
    q.on('data', (c) => ch.push(c))
    q.on('end', () => {
      const b = Buffer.concat(ch).toString('utf8')
      const u = new URL(q.url, 'http://stub')
      const p = u.pathname
      const Jx = (code, o) => { s.writeHead(code, { 'content-type': 'application/json' }); s.end(JSON.stringify(o)) }
      let j = {}
      try { j = JSON.parse(b) } catch (e) { j = Object.fromEntries(new URLSearchParams(b)) }
      const rec = (k, x) => { const c = Object.assign({ k }, x || {}); St.calls.push(c); return c }
      if (p.indexOf('/auth/v1/admin/users/') === 0) {
        rec('admin:' + q.method, { id: p.slice('/auth/v1/admin/users/'.length), key: q.headers.apikey, auth: q.headers.authorization })
        if (q.headers.apikey !== SERVICE) return Jx(401, { msg: 'stub: the admin API needs the service key' })
        if (St.adminFail) return Jx(500, { code: 500, error_code: 'unexpected_failure', msg: 'Database error deleting user' })
        return Jx(200, {})
      }
      if (p.indexOf('/auth/v1/') === 0) {
        if (q.headers.apikey !== ANON) { rec('auth-badkey', { p }); return Jx(401, { msg: 'bad apikey' }) }
        if (p === '/auth/v1/otp') {
          St.otps.push({ email: j.email, challenge: j.code_challenge, redirect_to: u.searchParams.get('redirect_to'), code: 'code-' + (St.otps.length + 1) })
          rec('otp', { email: j.email })
          return Jx(200, {})
        }
        if (p === '/auth/v1/token' && u.searchParams.get('grant_type') === 'pkce') {
          const o = St.otps.find((x) => x.code === j.auth_code)
          const ok = !!o && o.challenge === crypto.createHash('sha256').update(String(j.code_verifier || '')).digest('base64url')
          rec('token', { ok })
          if (!ok) return Jx(400, { code: 400, error_code: 'flow_state_not_found', msg: 'invalid flow state' })
          const us = user(o.email)
          return Jx(200, { access_token: 'at-' + us.id, token_type: 'bearer', expires_in: 3600, expires_at: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 3600, refresh_token: 'rt-' + us.id, user: us })
        }
        if (p === '/auth/v1/user' && St.userDown) { rec('user', { ok: false }); return Jx(500, { code: 500, msg: 'stub down' }) }
        if (p === '/auth/v1/user') { const us = Object.values(St.users).find((x) => q.headers.authorization === 'Bearer at-' + x.id); rec('user', { ok: !!us }); return us ? Jx(200, us) : Jx(401, { code: 401, error_code: 'bad_jwt', msg: 'invalid JWT' }) }
        if (p === '/auth/v1/logout') { rec('logout'); s.writeHead(204); return s.end() }
      }
      if (p.indexOf('/rest/v1/rpc/') === 0) {
        const fn = p.slice('/rest/v1/rpc/'.length)
        const c = rec('rpc:' + fn, { args: j, key: q.headers.apikey, profile: q.headers['content-profile'] })
        if (q.headers.apikey !== SERVICE || q.headers['content-profile'] !== 'protoolhub') return Jx(401, { message: 'stub: wrong key or schema' })
        if (St.rpcFail === fn) return Jx(500, { message: 'stub failure', code: 'XX000' })
        let out
        if (fn === 'claim_welcome') out = { outcome: 'already', free_credits: 0, paid_credits: 0 }
        else if (fn === 'polar_record_checkout') out = { outcome: St.record }
        else if (fn === 'polar_order_paid') out = St.paid || { outcome: 'credited', credits: 10, free_credits: 0, paid_credits: 10, deducted_credits: 0, shortfall_credits: St.alert, alert_shortfall: St.alert }
        else if (fn === 'polar_refund') out = { outcome: 'applied', deducted_credits: 2, shortfall_credits: St.alert, alert_shortfall: St.alert, free_credits: 0, paid_credits: 0 }
        else if (fn === 'polar_mark_alerted') out = { alerted_shortfall: j.p_shortfall }
        else if (fn === 'account_overview') out = St.overview || { free_credits: 2, paid_credits: 30, purchases: [], conversions: [] }
        else if (fn === 'delete_account') out = St.del || { outcome: 'deleted', keep_auth_user: false }
        else if (fn === 'polar_checkout_status') out = St.status || { status: 'pending', credits: 30, free_credits: 1, paid_credits: 0 }
        else return Jx(404, { message: 'no such function' })
        c.res = out
        return Jx(200, out)
      }
      if (p === '/polar/v1/checkouts/' && q.method === 'POST') {
        St.n++
        rec('polar:checkout', { auth: q.headers.authorization, version: q.headers['polar-version'], body: j, raw: b })
        if (St.polarDown) return Jx(500, { error: 'stub down' })
        return Jx(201, { id: 'chk_stub_' + St.n, url: 'https://sandbox.polar.sh/checkout/polar_c_stub_' + St.n, status: 'open' })
      }
      if (p === '/resend/emails' && q.method === 'POST') {
        rec('resend', { auth: q.headers.authorization, body: j })
        if (St.resendDown) return Jx(500, { message: 'stub down' })
        return Jx(200, { id: 'email-stub-1' })
      }
      rec('other', { p })
      Jx(404, { error: { message: 'unknown' } })
    })
  })
  return { St, server }
}

async function boot(o) {
  if (!fs.existsSync('.next/BUILD_ID')) throw new Error('.next has no build; criterion 3 builds it')
  const st = await stub()
  await new Promise((r, j) => { st.server.once('error', j); st.server.listen(o.stubPort, '127.0.0.1', r) })
  const stubUrl = 'http://127.0.0.1:' + o.stubPort
  const env = Object.assign({}, process.env, {
    OPENAI_API_KEY: 'stub', OPENAI_BASE_URL: stubUrl + '/v1', TURNSTILE_SITEVERIFY_URL: stubUrl + '/turnstile/v0/siteverify',
    SUPABASE_URL: stubUrl, SUPABASE_ANON_KEY: ANON, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY: SERVICE, ABUSE_HASH_SECRET: 'hash-stub-secret',
    POLAR_ACCESS_TOKEN: PTOKEN, POLAR_WEBHOOK_SECRET: WH, POLAR_PRODUCT_10: PROD.p10, POLAR_PRODUCT_30: PROD.p30, POLAR_PRODUCT_75: PROD.p75,
    RESEND_API_KEY: RKEY, OPERATOR_ALERT_EMAIL: ALERT_TO, POLAR_API_BASE_URL: stubUrl + '/polar', RESEND_API_BASE_URL: stubUrl + '/resend',
  })
  // Empty, not deleted: next start loads .env.local, which never overrides a variable that is already set.
  for (const k of ['VERCEL_ENV', 'TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY', 'UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_URL', 'UPSTASH_REDIS_REST_TOKEN', 'KV_REST_API_URL', 'KV_REST_API_TOKEN']) env[k] = ''
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(o.env || {})) env[k] = v === null ? '' : String(v).split('{STUB}').join(stubUrl)
  const kid = spawn(process.execPath, [path.join('node_modules', 'next', 'dist', 'bin', 'next'), 'start', '-p', String(o.port)], { env, stdio: 'ignore' })
  const close = async () => {
    if (kid.exitCode === null) { const done = new Promise((r) => kid.once('exit', r)); try { kid.kill() } catch (e) {} await Promise.race([done, sleep(5000)]) }
    st.server.closeAllConnections()
    await new Promise((r) => st.server.close(() => r()))
  }
  const base = 'http://127.0.0.1:' + o.port
  try { await until(async () => kid.exitCode === null && (await fetch(base + '/api/health')).status === 200, 90000, 'next start') } catch (e) { await close(); throw e }
  return { base, S: st.St, stubUrl, close }
}

async function signIn(ctx, email) {
  const jar = new Jar()
  const r = jar.take(await fetch(ctx.base + '/api/auth/email', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json', 'x-real-ip': '198.51.100.9' }, body: JSON.stringify({ email, next: '/workshop' }) }))
  if (r.status !== 200) throw new Error('POST /api/auth/email answered ' + r.status)
  const o = ctx.S.otps[ctx.S.otps.length - 1]
  if (!o || o.email.toLowerCase() !== email.toLowerCase()) throw new Error('the stub saw no otp request for ' + email)
  const cb = new URL(o.redirect_to)
  cb.searchParams.set('code', o.code)
  const f = jar.take(await fetch(ctx.base + cb.pathname + cb.search, { redirect: 'manual', headers: { cookie: jar.header(), 'x-real-ip': '198.51.100.9' } }))
  if (!(f.status >= 300 && f.status < 400)) throw new Error('the callback answered ' + f.status)
  return { jar, id: ctx.S.users[o.email].id }
}
```
