const SPEC23 = '.mavci/tasks/0023-polar-credits.md'
const MIG24 = 'supabase/migrations/20261029000000_protoolhub_account.sql'
const LIVE24 = 'supabase/checks/0024_live_catalog.sql'
const DOC24 = 'docs/0024-operator-steps.md'
const F24 = ['delete_account', 'account_overview']
const API24 = API.concat(['delete_account(uuid)', 'account_overview(uuid)'])
const CATS = ['cartoon', 'pet', 'avatar', 'line', 'drawing', 'paint', 'print', 'paper', 'textile', 'sculpt', 'caricature', 'graphic', 'era', 'surface']
const sha = (b) => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(b).digest('hex')
const headBuf = (f) => { const r = spawnSync('git', ['show', 'HEAD:' + f], { maxBuffer: 1 << 28 }); return r.status === 0 ? r.stdout : null }
const headTxt = (f) => { const b = headBuf(f); return b === null ? null : b.toString('utf8') }
const eq = (a, b) => JSON.stringify(a) === JSON.stringify(b)
const imp = async (rel) => import(pathToFileURL(path.resolve(rel)).href)
async function impHead(rel, name) {
  const d = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'h24-'))
  const f = path.join(d, name)
  fs.writeFileSync(f, headTxt(rel))
  return import(pathToFileURL(f).href)
}

// §5.2's table, parsed from the approved spec.
function styleRows() {
  const t = fs.readFileSync(SPEC, 'utf8').split('\r\n').join(LF)
  const a = t.indexOf('<!-- styles0024:start -->')
  const z = t.indexOf('<!-- styles0024:end -->')
  if (a < 0 || z < 0) throw new Error('the styles0024 table is missing from the spec')
  return t.slice(a, z).split(LF).filter((l) => l.indexOf('| ') === 0).map((l) => l.split('|').slice(1, -1).map((x) => x.trim())).filter((c) => c[0] !== 'id')
    .map((c) => ({ id: c[0], category: c[1], subject: c[2], trn: c[3], trd: c[4], enn: c[5], end: c[6], body: c[7] }))
}
// §5.1's table: key -> [tr, en].
function textRows51() {
  const t = fs.readFileSync(SPEC, 'utf8').split('\r\n').join(LF)
  const a = t.indexOf('### 5.1 ')
  const z = t.indexOf('### 5.2 ', a)
  const out = {}
  for (const l of t.slice(a, z).split(LF)) {
    if (l.indexOf('| ') !== 0) continue
    const c = l.split(' | ')
    if (c.length !== 3) continue
    const k = c[0].slice(2).trim()
    if (!/^[a-zA-Z]+[.][A-Za-z_]+$/.test(k)) continue
    out[k] = [c[1].trim(), c[2].replace(/ [|]$/, '').trim()]
  }
  return out
}
const pick = (o, k) => k.split('.').reduce((x, y) => (x == null ? undefined : x[y]), o)

async function pg3(after) {
  const r22 = R(MIG22)
  const r23 = R(MIG)
  const r24 = R(MIG24)
  if (r22 === null || r23 === null) throw new Error('an applied migration is missing')
  if (r24 === null) throw new Error(MIG24 + ' is missing')
  const { PGlite } = req('@electric-sql/pglite')
  const db = new PGlite()
  await db.exec(PRELUDE)
  const before = await outside(db)
  await db.exec(r22)
  await db.exec(r23)
  const mid = await snapAll(db)
  await db.exec(r24)
  if (after) await db.exec(after)
  return { db, before, mid }
}

// The 12 tables and the 11 functions as the catalog sees them.
async function snapAll(db) {
  const q = async (s) => (await db.query(s)).rows
  return {
    rel: await q("select c.relname, c.relkind::text k, c.relacl::text acl, c.relrowsecurity rls from pg_class c where c.relnamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace order by 1"),
    col: await q("select c.relname || '.' || a.attname n, format_type(a.atttypid, a.atttypmod) t, a.attnotnull nn, pg_get_expr(d.adbin, d.adrelid) def from pg_attribute a join pg_class c on c.oid = a.attrelid left join pg_attrdef d on d.adrelid = a.attrelid and d.adnum = a.attnum where c.relnamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace and c.relkind = 'r' and a.attnum > 0 and not a.attisdropped order by 1"),
    con: await q("select c.relname || '.' || con.conname n, pg_get_constraintdef(con.oid) d from pg_constraint con join pg_class c on c.oid = con.conrelid where c.relnamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace order by 1"),
    idx: await q("select indexrelid::regclass::text n, pg_get_indexdef(indexrelid) d from pg_index i join pg_class c on c.oid = i.indrelid where c.relnamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace order by 1"),
    fn: await q("select p.oid::regprocedure::text sig, p.proname, pg_get_functiondef(p.oid) d, p.proacl::text acl, p.prosecdef, p.proconfig::text cfg from pg_proc p where p.pronamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace order by 1"),
    nsp: await q("select nspacl::text from pg_namespace where nspname = 'protoolhub'"),
  }
}

// One function's text in a migration, from `create [or replace] function protoolhub.<n>(` to its `$$;`.
function fnIn(sql, n) {
  const s = sql.split('\r\n').join(LF)
  let a = s.indexOf('create or replace function protoolhub.' + n + '(')
  if (a < 0) a = s.indexOf('create function protoolhub.' + n + '(')
  if (a < 0) return null
  const z = s.indexOf('$$;' + LF, s.indexOf('as $$', a)) + 4
  return s.slice(a, z)
}
const unguard = (s) => s.split(LF).reduce((o, l) => { if (l.indexOf('-- 0024 guard start') >= 0) o.skip = true; if (!o.skip) o.out.push(l); if (l.indexOf('-- 0024 guard end') >= 0) o.skip = false; return o }, { out: [], skip: false }).out.join(LF).replace('create or replace function', 'create function')

C['127'] = async (bad) => {
  const dir = 'supabase/migrations'
  const files = (fs.existsSync(dir) ? fs.readdirSync(dir) : []).sort()
  if (files.join() !== [MIG22, MIG, MIG24].map((x) => path.basename(x)).join()) bad.push(dir + ' holds [' + files.join(', ') + '], expected exactly the 0022, 0023 and 0024 files')
  const r22 = R(MIG22)
  if (r22 === null || r22.split('\r\n').join(LF).trim() !== block('sql migration-0022', SPEC22).trim()) bad.push(MIG22 + ' (applied) was changed')
  const r23 = R(MIG)
  if (r23 === null || r23.split('\r\n').join(LF).trim() !== block('sql migration-0023', SPEC23).trim()) bad.push(MIG + ' (applied) was changed')
  const raw = R(MIG24)
  if (raw === null) { bad.push(MIG24 + ' is missing'); return }
  if (raw.split('\r\n').join(LF).trim() !== block('sql migration-0024').trim()) bad.push(MIG24 + ' differs from the migration-0024 block of the approved spec')
  const sql = stripSql(raw)
  const forbid = [
    [/\bpublic\s*\./i, 'names an object in schema public'],
    [/\bauth\s*\./i, 'names an object in schema auth'],
    [/\bcreate\s+(or\s+replace\s+)?(constraint\s+)?trigger\b/i, 'creates a trigger'],
    [/\btruncate\b/i, 'contains TRUNCATE'],
    [/(^|;)\s*(begin|commit|rollback|start\s+transaction)\s*;/i, 'manages its own transaction'],
    [/\bsecurity\s+invoker\b/i, 'declares SECURITY INVOKER'],
    [/\bgrant\b[^;]*\bto\b[^;]*\b(anon|authenticated|public)\b/i, 'grants something to anon, authenticated or PUBLIC'],
    [/\bcreate\s+policy\b/i, 'creates a policy'],
    [/\b(create|alter|drop)\s+role\b/i, 'manages roles'],
    [/\bset\s+role\b/i, 'switches role'],
    [/\bcreate\s+extension\b/i, 'creates an extension'],
    [/\bcreate\s+schema\b/i, 'creates a schema'],
    [/\bon\s+all\s+(tables|functions|sequences|routines)\s+in\s+schema\b/i, 'grants or revokes schema-wide'],
    [/\balter\s+default\s+privileges\b/i, 'alters default privileges'],
    [/\bwelcome_email_keys\b/i, 'names welcome_email_keys'],
    [/bptiwxuzscfhuyzpuwip/, 'names the other Supabase project'],
    [/\b(insert\s+into|update|delete\s+from)\s+leadgen\b/i, 'writes to leadgen'],
  ]
  for (const [re, what] of forbid) if (re.test(sql)) bad.push(MIG24 + ' ' + what)
  const lead = sql.match(/\bleadgen\b[.a-z_]*/gi) || []
  if (lead.length !== 1 || lead[0] !== 'leadgen.org_members' || !/select\s+exists\s*\(\s*select\s+1\s+from\s+leadgen\.org_members\s+m\s+where\s+m\.user_id\s*=\s*p_user_id\s*\)/i.test(sql)) bad.push(MIG24 + ' mentions leadgen other than one read of leadgen.org_members: ' + lead.join(', '))
  const drops = (sql.match(/\bdrop\b[^;]*/gi) || []).filter((d) => !/^drop\s+not\s+null$/i.test(d.trim()))
  if (drops.length !== 2 || drops.some((d) => !/^drop\s+constraint\s+polar_(checkouts|orders)_user_id_fkey$/i.test(d.trim()))) bad.push(MIG24 + ' drops something other than the two user_id foreign keys: ' + drops.join(' | '))
  const dels = sql.match(/\bdelete\s+from\s+[a-z0-9_.]+/gi) || []
  if (dels.length !== 1 || !/protoolhub\.profiles$/i.test(dels[0])) bad.push(MIG24 + ' deletes from something other than protoolhub.profiles: ' + dels.join(', '))
  const orr = [...sql.matchAll(/\bor\s+replace\s+function\s+([a-z0-9_.]+)/gi)].map((m) => m[1]).sort()
  if (orr.join() !== 'protoolhub.polar_order_paid,protoolhub.polar_reconcile') bad.push(MIG24 + ' replaces [' + orr.join(', ') + ']')
  const alters = [...sql.matchAll(/\balter\s+(table|function|index|sequence)\s+([a-z0-9_.]+)/gi)].map((m) => m[2])
  if (alters.some((x) => x !== 'protoolhub.polar_checkouts' && x !== 'protoolhub.polar_orders')) bad.push(MIG24 + ' alters ' + alters.join(', '))
  for (const m of sql.matchAll(/\bcreate\s+(?:or\s+replace\s+)?(?:unique\s+)?(table|function|view|materialized\s+view|sequence|type|procedure|index)\s+(?:if\s+not\s+exists\s+)?([a-z0-9_."]+)/gi)) if (!/^"?protoolhub"?\./i.test(m[2])) bad.push(MIG24 + ' creates ' + m[1] + ' ' + m[2] + ' outside protoolhub')
  return 'the approved SQL; 0022 and 0023 untouched; protoolhub only, one leadgen read'
}

C['128'] = async (bad) => {
  const { db, before, mid } = await pg3()
  const now = await snapAll(db)
  const rows = async (s) => (await db.query(s)).rows
  const t = now.rel.filter((r) => r.k === 'r')
  if (t.map((r) => r.relname).sort().join() !== [...TABLES].sort().join()) bad.push('protoolhub tables are [' + t.map((r) => r.relname).join(', ') + ']')
  for (const r of t) if (!r.rls) bad.push(r.relname + ' has RLS off')
  for (const role of ['anon', 'authenticated', 'service_role']) {
    const g = await rows("select c.relname from pg_class c where c.relnamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace and c.relkind in ('r', 'p', 'S', 'v', 'm') and (case when c.relkind = 'S' then has_sequence_privilege('" + role + "', c.oid, 'USAGE,SELECT,UPDATE') else has_table_privilege('" + role + "', c.oid, 'SELECT,INSERT,UPDATE,DELETE,TRUNCATE,REFERENCES,TRIGGER') end)")
    if (g.length) bad.push(role + ' holds a privilege on ' + g.map((x) => x.relname).join(', '))
  }
  const f = await rows("select p.oid::regprocedure::text sig, p.proname, p.prosecdef, p.proconfig, has_function_privilege('anon', p.oid, 'EXECUTE') a, has_function_privilege('authenticated', p.oid, 'EXECUTE') u, has_function_privilege('service_role', p.oid, 'EXECUTE') s, (p.proacl is null or exists (select 1 from aclexplode(p.proacl) x where x.grantee = 0 and x.privilege_type = 'EXECUTE')) pub from pg_proc p where p.pronamespace = 'protoolhub'::regnamespace")
  const want = F22.concat(['polar_reconcile', 'polar_record_checkout', 'polar_order_paid', 'polar_refund', 'polar_mark_alerted', 'polar_checkout_status'], F24).sort()
  if (f.map((x) => x.proname).sort().join() !== want.join()) bad.push('protoolhub functions are [' + f.map((x) => x.proname).sort().join(', ') + ']')
  for (const x of f) {
    if (!x.prosecdef) bad.push(x.sig + ' is not SECURITY DEFINER')
    if (JSON.stringify(x.proconfig) !== JSON.stringify(['search_path=""'])) bad.push(x.sig + ' search_path is ' + JSON.stringify(x.proconfig))
    if (x.a || x.u || x.pub) bad.push(x.sig + ' is executable by anon, authenticated or PUBLIC')
  }
  const svc = f.filter((x) => x.s).map((x) => x.sig.replace(/^protoolhub[.]/, '').split(' ').join('')).sort()
  if (svc.join() !== [...API24].sort().join()) bad.push('service_role can execute [' + svc.join(', ') + ']')
  if ((await rows("select 1 from pg_policies where schemaname = 'protoolhub'")).length) bad.push('a policy exists')
  for (const tb of ['polar_checkouts', 'polar_orders']) {
    const c = now.con.find((x) => x.n === tb + '.' + tb + '_user_id_fkey')
    if (!c || c.d !== 'FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES protoolhub.profiles(user_id) ON DELETE SET NULL') bad.push(tb + '.user_id foreign key is ' + (c && c.d))
    const k = now.col.find((x) => x.n === tb + '.user_id')
    if (!k || k.nn) bad.push(tb + '.user_id is still NOT NULL')
  }
  const strip = (s, drop) => s.filter((x) => drop.indexOf(x.n || x.sig) < 0)
  const CH = ['polar_checkouts.user_id', 'polar_orders.user_id', 'polar_checkouts.polar_checkouts_user_id_fkey', 'polar_orders.polar_orders_user_id_fkey', 'polar_checkouts.polar_checkouts_user_id_not_null', 'polar_orders.polar_orders_user_id_not_null']
  if (!eq(strip(mid.col, CH), strip(now.col, CH))) bad.push('a column other than the two user_id columns changed')
  const cd = (a, b) => a.filter((x) => !b.some((y) => eq(x, y))).map((x) => x.n + ' ' + x.d)
  const c0 = strip(mid.con, CH), c1 = strip(now.con, CH)
  if (!eq(c0, c1)) bad.push('a constraint other than the two user_id foreign keys changed: ' + cd(c0, c1).concat(cd(c1, c0)).join(', '))
  if (!eq(mid.idx, now.idx) || !eq(mid.rel, now.rel) || !eq(mid.nsp, now.nsp)) bad.push('an index, table privilege or the schema ACL changed')
  const RE = ['protoolhub.polar_reconcile(text)', 'protoolhub.polar_order_paid(text,text,text,text,text,integer,text)']
  const old = mid.fn.filter((x) => RE.indexOf(x.sig.split(' ').join('')) < 0)
  const kept = now.fn.filter((x) => old.some((o) => o.sig === x.sig))
  if (!eq(old, kept)) bad.push('a 0022 or 0023 function other than the two re-created ones changed')
  for (const s of RE) { const a = mid.fn.find((x) => x.sig.split(' ').join('') === s), b = now.fn.find((x) => x.sig.split(' ').join('') === s); if (!a || !b || a.acl !== b.acl || a.prosecdef !== b.prosecdef || a.cfg !== b.cfg) bad.push(s + ' lost its privileges or settings') }
  const raw = R(MIG24) || ''
  for (const n of ['polar_reconcile', 'polar_order_paid']) {
    const mine = fnIn(raw, n)
    const theirs = fnIn(block('sql migration-0023', SPEC23), n)
    if (!mine || mine.indexOf('-- 0024 guard start') < 0) bad.push(n + ' has no 0024 guard')
    else if (unguard(mine) !== theirs) bad.push(n + ' differs from 0023 by more than the guard lines')
  }
  if ((await outside(db)) !== before) bad.push('something outside protoolhub changed')
  return '12 tables, 13 functions, 11 entry points, user links set null, everything else as after 0023'
}


C['129'] = async (bad) => {
  const { db } = await pg3()
  const E = (n) => UID(n)
  await users(db, [[1, 'a.b@gmail.com'], [2, 'two@example.com'], [3, 'member@example.com'], [4, 'dash@example.com'], [6, 'other@example.com']])
  await db.query("insert into leadgen.org_members (org_id, user_id, role) values ($1, $2, 'owner')", [UID(900), E(3)])
  const lead0 = JSON.stringify((await db.query('select * from leadgen.org_members order by user_id')).rows)
  for (const n of [1, 2, 3, 4, 6]) { const w = await S(db, 'claim_welcome', [E(n), 'cartoonify', H64('ip' + n), H64('dev' + n)]); if (w.outcome !== 'granted') bad.push('user ' + n + ' welcome ' + w.outcome) }
  const conv = async (n, style, ok) => { const b = await S(db, 'begin_conversion', [E(n), 'cartoonify', style]); if (!b.ok) throw new Error('no credit for user ' + n); if (ok) await S(db, 'complete_conversion', [b.conversion_id]); else await S(db, 'refund_conversion', [b.conversion_id, 'test']); return b }
  await conv(1, 'classic', true)
  await conv(1, 'bold-ink', false)
  const buy = async (n, co, ord, pack, credits, net) => {
    const r = await S(db, 'polar_record_checkout', [co, E(n), 'production', 'prod-' + credits, pack, credits])
    if (r.outcome !== 'recorded') bad.push(co + ' ' + r.outcome)
    const p = await S(db, 'polar_order_paid', ['production', ord, co, E(n), 'prod-' + credits, net, 'usd'])
    if (p.outcome !== 'credited') bad.push(ord + ' ' + p.outcome)
  }
  await buy(1, 'chk_a', 'ord_a', 'p10', 10, 399)
  await buy(1, 'chk_b', 'ord_b', 'p30', 30, 999)
  await buy(1, 'chk_c', 'ord_c', 'p75', 75, 1999)
  await S(db, 'polar_refund', ['production', 're_b1', 'ord_b', 100, true])
  await S(db, 'polar_refund', ['production', 'order:ord_c', 'ord_c', 1999, true])
  await S(db, 'polar_record_checkout', ['chk_open', E(1), 'production', 'prod-10', 'p10', 10])
  await conv(1, 'pet-oil-portrait', true)
  const ov = await S(db, 'account_overview', [E(1)])
  if (Object.keys(ov).sort().join() !== 'conversions,free_credits,paid_credits,purchases') bad.push('overview keys are ' + Object.keys(ov).join())
  if (ov.free_credits !== 1 || ov.paid_credits !== 35) bad.push('overview balance ' + ov.free_credits + '/' + ov.paid_credits + ', expected 1/35')
  const pur = ov.purchases || []
  if (!eq(pur.map((x) => [x.pack, x.credits, x.refund]), [['p75', 75, 'full'], ['p30', 30, 'partial'], ['p10', 10, 'none']])) bad.push('purchases are ' + JSON.stringify(pur.map((x) => [x.pack, x.credits, x.refund])))
  if (pur.some((x) => Object.keys(x).sort().join() !== 'at,credits,pack,refund')) bad.push('a purchase has keys ' + Object.keys(pur[0] || {}).join())
  const cv = ov.conversions || []
  if (!eq(cv.map((x) => [x.style, x.kind]), [['pet-oil-portrait', 'paid'], ['classic', 'free']])) bad.push('conversions are ' + JSON.stringify(cv.map((x) => [x.style, x.kind])))
  if (cv.some((x) => Object.keys(x).sort().join() !== 'at,kind,style')) bad.push('a conversion has keys ' + Object.keys(cv[0] || {}).join())
  if (/ord_|chk_|399|999/.test(JSON.stringify(ov))) bad.push('the overview leaks an order id, checkout id or amount')
  const none = await S(db, 'account_overview', [UID(99)])
  if (none.free_credits !== 0 || none.paid_credits !== 0 || !eq(none.purchases, []) || !eq(none.conversions, [])) bad.push('an unknown user gets ' + JSON.stringify(none))
  if ((await one(db, 'select count(*)::int n from protoolhub.profiles where user_id = $1', [UID(99)])).n) bad.push('the overview created a profile')
  // A running conversion blocks deletion and changes nothing.
  await S(db, 'begin_conversion', [E(2), 'cartoonify', 'classic'])
  let d = await S(db, 'delete_account', [E(2)])
  if (d.outcome !== 'busy') bad.push('a fresh reservation: ' + JSON.stringify(d))
  if (!(await one(db, 'select count(*)::int n from protoolhub.profiles where user_id = $1', [E(2)])).n) bad.push('busy still deleted the profile')
  await db.query("update protoolhub.conversions set created_at = now() - interval '11 minutes' where user_id = $1", [E(2)])
  d = await S(db, 'delete_account', [E(2)])
  if (d.outcome !== 'deleted') bad.push('a stale reservation still blocks: ' + JSON.stringify(d))
  const w6 = JSON.stringify(await one(db, 'select free_credits, paid_credits from protoolhub.wallets where user_id = $1', [E(6)]))
  const ledgerN = (await one(db, 'select count(*)::int n from protoolhub.credit_ledger')).n
  // The real deletion.
  d = await S(db, 'delete_account', [E(1)])
  if (d.outcome !== 'deleted' || d.keep_auth_user !== false) bad.push('deleting user 1: ' + JSON.stringify(d))
  for (const tb of ['profiles', 'wallets', 'credit_ledger', 'conversions', 'welcome_grants', 'sandbox_testers']) if ((await one(db, 'select count(*)::int n from protoolhub.' + tb + ' where user_id = $1', [E(1)])).n) bad.push(tb + ' still holds user 1')
  if (!(await one(db, 'select count(*)::int n from protoolhub.welcome_email_keys where email_key = $1', [H64('ab@gmail.com')])).n) bad.push('the welcome e-mail hash was lost')
  const ords = (await db.query("select order_id, user_id, net_amount, refunded_amount, credits from protoolhub.polar_orders where order_id in ('ord_a', 'ord_b', 'ord_c') order by 1")).rows
  if (!eq(ords.map((o) => [o.order_id, o.user_id, o.net_amount, o.refunded_amount, o.credits]), [['ord_a', null, 399, 0, 10], ['ord_b', null, 999, 100, 30], ['ord_c', null, 1999, 1999, 75]])) bad.push('the orders after deletion are ' + JSON.stringify(ords))
  const op = await one(db, "select user_id, status, refuse_reason from protoolhub.polar_checkouts where checkout_id = 'chk_open'")
  if (!op || op.user_id !== null || op.status !== 'refused' || op.refuse_reason !== 'account_deleted') bad.push('the open checkout after deletion is ' + JSON.stringify(op))
  if ((await one(db, 'select count(*)::int n from protoolhub.polar_checkouts where user_id = $1', [E(1)])).n) bad.push('a checkout still names user 1')
  const lr = await S(db, 'polar_refund', ['production', 're_a1', 'ord_a', 200, true])
  if (lr.outcome !== 'account_deleted' || lr.alert_shortfall !== 0) bad.push('a refund after deletion: ' + JSON.stringify(lr))
  const oa = await one(db, "select refunded_amount, deducted_credits, shortfall_credits from protoolhub.polar_orders where order_id = 'ord_a'")
  if (!oa || oa.refunded_amount !== 200 || oa.deducted_credits !== 0 || oa.shortfall_credits !== 0) bad.push('ord_a after the late refund is ' + JSON.stringify(oa))
  const lp = await S(db, 'polar_order_paid', ['production', 'ord_late', 'chk_open', E(1), 'prod-10', 399, 'usd'])
  if (lp.outcome !== 'account_deleted' || lp.credits !== 0) bad.push('a late order.paid: ' + JSON.stringify(lp))
  d = await S(db, 'delete_account', [E(1)])
  if (d.outcome !== 'nothing') bad.push('a second deletion: ' + JSON.stringify(d))
  const back = await S(db, 'claim_welcome', [E(1), 'cartoonify', H64('ipz'), H64('devz')])
  if (back.outcome !== 'email_used' || back.free_credits !== 0) bad.push('the same address back: ' + JSON.stringify(back))
  await users(db, [[5, ' A.B+x@GoogleMail.com ']])
  const alias = await S(db, 'claim_welcome', [E(5), 'cartoonify', H64('ipy'), H64('devy')])
  if (alias.outcome !== 'email_used') bad.push('an alias of the deleted address: ' + alias.outcome)
  // A Firma Bulucu member keeps the sign-in.
  d = await S(db, 'delete_account', [E(3)])
  if (d.outcome !== 'deleted' || d.keep_auth_user !== true) bad.push('deleting a member: ' + JSON.stringify(d))
  if (JSON.stringify((await db.query('select * from leadgen.org_members order by user_id')).rows) !== lead0) bad.push('leadgen.org_members changed')
  // The auth user deleted directly (dashboard): records anonymised by the foreign keys.
  await buy(4, 'chk_d2', 'ord_d4', 'p10', 10, 399)
  await S(db, 'polar_record_checkout', ['chk_d', E(4), 'production', 'prod-10', 'p10', 10])
  await db.query('delete from auth.users where id = $1', [E(4)])
  const od = await one(db, "select user_id from protoolhub.polar_orders where order_id = 'ord_d4'")
  if (!od || od.user_id !== null) bad.push('a dashboard deletion left the order ' + JSON.stringify(od))
  let late
  try { late = await S(db, 'polar_order_paid', ['production', 'ord_d', 'chk_d', E(4), 'prod-10', 399, 'usd']) } catch (e) { bad.push('order.paid on an anonymised open checkout raised: ' + e.message) }
  if (late && (late.outcome !== 'account_deleted' || late.credits !== 0)) bad.push('order.paid on an anonymised open checkout: ' + JSON.stringify(late))
  const cd = await one(db, "select status, refuse_reason from protoolhub.polar_checkouts where checkout_id = 'chk_d'")
  if (!cd || cd.status !== 'refused' || cd.refuse_reason !== 'account_deleted') bad.push('chk_d is ' + JSON.stringify(cd))
  // Bystanders and roles.
  if (JSON.stringify(await one(db, 'select free_credits, paid_credits from protoolhub.wallets where user_id = $1', [E(6)])) !== w6) bad.push('another user\'s wallet changed')
  await ledgerOk(db, bad, 6)
  if ((await one(db, 'select count(*)::int n from protoolhub.credit_ledger where user_id is null')).n) bad.push('a ledger row without a user')
  if ((await one(db, 'select count(*)::int n from protoolhub.credit_ledger')).n > ledgerN + 2) bad.push('deletions wrote ledger rows')
  for (const role of ['anon', 'authenticated']) for (const fn of F24) if (!(await denied(db, role, 'select protoolhub.' + fn + "('" + E(6) + "')"))) bad.push(role + ' can call ' + fn)
  if (!(await throws(() => S(db, 'delete_account', [null])))) bad.push('delete_account(null) did not raise')
  return 'overview, busy, deletion keeps the hash and anonymises orders, late refund and order.paid, member kept, dashboard path'
}

C['130'] = async (bad) => {
  const spec = block('sql live-check-0024')
  const file = R(LIVE24)
  if (file === null) bad.push(LIVE24 + ' is missing')
  else if (file.split('\r\n').join(LF).trim() !== spec.trim()) bad.push(LIVE24 + ' differs from the live-check-0024 block')
  const body = spec.replace(/--[^\n]*/g, ' ').replace(/'[^']*'/g, "''")
  if (!/^\s*select\b/i.test(body)) bad.push('the live check does not start with SELECT')
  if ((body.match(/;/g) || []).length !== 1 || !/;\s*$/.test(body)) bad.push('the live check is not exactly one statement')
  const w = /\b(insert|update|delete|merge|create|alter|drop|grant|revoke|truncate|call|do|copy|vacuum|analyze|lock|set|reset|comment|refresh|notify|listen|prepare|execute)\b/i.exec(body)
  if (w) bad.push('the live check contains the word ' + w[1])
  const probe = async (after) => { const { db } = await pg3(after); return (await db.query(spec)).rows }
  const clean = await probe()
  if (clean.length) bad.push('on the three migrations the live check reports: ' + clean.map((r) => r.problem).join(' | '))
  const old = fnIn(block('sql migration-0023', SPEC23), 'polar_reconcile').replace('create function', 'create or replace function')
  const V = [
    ['alter table protoolhub.polar_orders drop constraint polar_orders_user_id_fkey; alter table protoolhub.polar_orders add constraint polar_orders_user_id_fkey foreign key (user_id) references protoolhub.profiles (user_id) on delete cascade', 'orders cascading again'],
    ['alter table protoolhub.polar_checkouts alter column user_id set not null', 'a NOT NULL user link'],
    ['grant execute on function protoolhub.delete_account(uuid) to anon', 'anon able to delete accounts'],
    ['revoke execute on function protoolhub.account_overview(uuid) from service_role', 'the overview not callable'],
    [old, 'reconcile without the guard'],
    ['alter function protoolhub.delete_account(uuid) security invoker', 'an invoker delete_account'],
    ['alter function protoolhub.account_overview(uuid) reset search_path', 'the overview without the empty search_path'],
    ["create function protoolhub.extra() returns int language sql security definer set search_path = '' as 'select 1'; revoke all on function protoolhub.extra() from public", 'an unexpected function'],
    ['alter table protoolhub.polar_orders disable row level security', 'RLS off'],
    ['create policy p on protoolhub.polar_orders for select to authenticated using (true)', 'a policy'],
    ['alter table leadgen.org_members rename column user_id to member_id', 'org_members without user_id'],
    ['alter table protoolhub.welcome_email_keys add column user_id uuid references protoolhub.profiles (user_id) on delete cascade', 'the e-mail hash tied to an account'],
  ]
  for (const [sql, what] of V) if (!(await probe(sql)).length) bad.push('the live check does not report ' + what)
  return 'read-only, silent on three migrations, loud on ' + V.length + ' sabotaged variants'
}


/* ------------------------------------------------- static code and data */

const ROUTES24 = ['app/api/session/route.ts', 'app/api/account/route.ts', 'app/api/account/delete/route.ts']
const PAGES24 = ['app/(tr)/account/page.tsx', 'app/(en)/en/account/page.tsx']

C['120'] = async (bad) => {
  const TR = await dict('tr')
  for (const f of ROUTES24) {
    const s = R(f)
    if (s === null) { bad.push(f + ' is missing'); continue }
    if (!/export const dynamic = ['"]force-dynamic['"]/.test(s)) bad.push(f + ' is not force-dynamic')
    if (!/export const runtime = ['"]nodejs['"]/.test(s)) bad.push(f + ' is not nodejs')
    if (s.indexOf('no-store') < 0) bad.push(f + ' does not answer no-store')
    if (/\bfrom ['"](node:)?fs['"]/.test(s)) bad.push(f + ' touches the file system')
  }
  const ex = (f, m) => new RegExp('export (async )?function ' + m + '\\b').test(R(f) || '')
  if (ex(ROUTES24[0], 'POST') || !ex(ROUTES24[0], 'GET')) bad.push('/api/session must serve GET only')
  if (ex(ROUTES24[1], 'POST') || !ex(ROUTES24[1], 'GET')) bad.push('/api/account must serve GET only')
  if (ex(ROUTES24[2], 'GET') || !ex(ROUTES24[2], 'POST')) bad.push('/api/account/delete must serve POST only')
  const code = walk('app').concat(walk('components'), walk('lib')).filter((p) => /[.](ts|tsx)$/.test(p))
  for (const f of code) {
    const s = fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8')
    if (f !== 'lib/supabase/server.ts' && /\bdeleteUser\b|admin[/]users/.test(s)) bad.push(f + ' uses the Auth admin API')
    if (/auth[.]users|from\(['"]users['"]\)|\bleadgen\b/i.test(s)) bad.push(f + ' names auth.users or leadgen')
    if (f !== 'lib/env.ts' && /process[.]env/.test(s)) bad.push(f + ' reads process.env')
    if (f !== 'lib/env.ts' && f !== 'lib/supabase/server.ts' && /SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY|serviceKey/.test(s)) bad.push(f + ' handles the service key')
    if (f.indexOf('components/') === 0 && /from ['"]@[/]lib[/](env|supabase[/]server|account)['"]/.test(s) && !/from ['"]@[/]lib[/]account['"]/.test(s.replace(/[^\n]*@[/]lib[/](env|supabase)[^\n]*/g, ''))) bad.push(f + ' imports a server module')
    if (f.indexOf('components/') === 0 && /@[/]lib[/](env|supabase)/.test(s)) bad.push(f + ' imports lib/env or lib/supabase')
  }
  const srv = R('lib/supabase/server.ts') || ''
  if (!/auth[.]admin[.]deleteUser\(/.test(srv)) bad.push('lib/supabase/server.ts does not call auth.admin.deleteUser')
  for (const fn of ['accountOverview', 'deleteAccountData', 'deleteAuthUser']) if (!new RegExp('export async function ' + fn + '\\b').test(srv)) bad.push('lib/supabase/server.ts does not export ' + fn)
  if (!/rpc\(['"]delete_account['"]/.test(srv) || !/rpc\(['"]account_overview['"]/.test(srv)) bad.push('the two RPCs are not called from lib/supabase/server.ts')
  const acc = R('lib/account.ts')
  if (acc === null) bad.push('lib/account.ts is missing')
  else {
    if (/^import /m.test(acc) || /process[.]env|fetch\(|from ['"]next/.test(acc)) bad.push('lib/account.ts is not pure')
    const A = await imp('lib/account.ts')
    for (const k of ['CONFIRM_REQUIRED', 'CONVERSION_IN_PROGRESS']) if (!A.ACCOUNT_ERROR_MESSAGES || A.ACCOUNT_ERROR_MESSAGES[k] !== TR.errors[k]) bad.push('ACCOUNT_ERROR_MESSAGES.' + k + ' is not tr.errors.' + k)
    if (typeof A.isDeleteConfirmation !== 'function' || A.isDeleteConfirmation({ confirm: 'DELETE' }) !== true || [null, {}, { confirm: true }, { confirm: 'delete' }, 'DELETE', { confirm: ['DELETE'] }].some((b) => A.isDeleteConfirmation(b) !== false)) bad.push('isDeleteConfirmation is wrong')
    if (typeof A.refundState !== 'function' || A.refundState(399, 0) !== 'none' || A.refundState(399, 1) !== 'partial' || A.refundState(399, 399) !== 'full' || A.refundState(399, 500) !== 'full') bad.push('refundState is wrong')
  }
  for (const f of PAGES24) {
    const s = R(f)
    if (s === null) { bad.push(f + ' is missing'); continue }
    if (!/robots:\s*\{[^}]*index:\s*false/.test(s)) bad.push(f + ' is not noindex')
    if (s.indexOf('AccountPage') < 0) bad.push(f + ' does not render AccountPage')
  }
  if (/account/.test(R('app/sitemap.ts') || '')) bad.push('the sitemap lists the account page')
  const P = await imp('lib/i18n/paths.ts')
  if (P.counterpartPath('/account') !== '/en/account' || P.counterpartPath('/en/account') !== '/account') bad.push('lib/i18n/paths.ts has no /account pair')
  const hd = R('components/site-header.tsx') || ''
  if (!/<ProfileLink\b/.test(hd) || hd.indexOf('site-nav') > hd.indexOf('<ProfileLink') || hd.indexOf('<ProfileLink') > hd.indexOf('</nav>')) bad.push('site-header does not render ProfileLink inside .site-nav')
  const pl = R('components/profile-link.tsx') || ''
  if (pl.indexOf('/api/session') < 0 || pl.indexOf('site-nav-profile') < 0 || pl.indexOf("'/account'") < 0) bad.push('components/profile-link.tsx does not call /api/session and link /account with .site-nav-profile')
  const chunks = walk('.next/static').filter((p) => p.endsWith('.js'))
  if (!chunks.length) bad.push('.next/static has no chunks; criterion 3 builds them')
  for (const f of chunks) { const s = fs.readFileSync(f, 'utf8'); for (const w of ['admin/users', 'deleteUser', 'delete_account', 'account_overview', 'SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY']) if (s.indexOf(w) >= 0) bad.push(f + ' contains ' + w) }
  return 'routes, pure lib/account.ts, admin API only in the server module, pages noindex, the header link'
}

C['125'] = async (bad) => {
  const J0 = (f) => JSON.parse(headTxt(f) || '{}')

  const wm0 = J0('lib/style-web-manifest.json'), wm = J1('lib/style-web-manifest.json')

    for (const d of ['public/styles/', 'public/styles-web/']) { const b0 = headBuf(d + id + '.webp'); if (!b0 || !fs.existsSync(d + id + '.webp') || !b0.equals(fs.readFileSync(d + id + '.webp'))) bad.push(d + id + '.webp is not byte for byte HEAD') }
    if (!eq((pm.previews || {})[id], (pm0.previews || {})[id])) bad.push(id + ': its preview record changed')
    if (!eq((wm.files || {})[id], wm0.files[id])) bad.push(id + ': its web copy record changed')
  }
  for (const k of Object.keys(pm0.trials || {})) if (!eq(pm.trials[k], pm0.trials[k])) bad.push('trial ' + k + ' changed')
  if (!eq((pm.discarded || []).slice(0, (pm0.discarded || []).length), pm0.discarded || [])) bad.push('the discarded records of earlier tasks changed')
  for (const k of Object.keys(pm0)) if (['trials', 'discarded', 'previews', 'last_error'].indexOf(k) < 0 && !eq(pm[k], pm0[k])) bad.push('preview manifest key ' + k + ' changed')
  const rows = styleRows()
  if (rows.length !== 20) bad.push('§5.2 has ' + rows.length + ' rows')
  const ch = fs.existsSync('reports/0024-choices.json') ? JSON.parse(R('reports/0024-choices.json')) : null
  if (!ch) { bad.push('reports/0024-choices.json is missing'); return }
  const cs = await imp('lib/cartoon-styles.ts')
  const sp = await imp('lib/style-previews.ts')
  for (const r of rows) {
    const c = (ch.newStyles || {})[r.id]
    if (!c || c.choice !== 'A' || c.subject !== r.subject) { bad.push(r.id + ': the choices say ' + JSON.stringify(c && [c.choice, c.subject])); continue }
    const p = (pm.previews || {})[r.id] || {}
    const f = 'public/styles/' + r.id + '.webp'
    if (p.task !== '0024' || p.from_trial !== r.id + '.A.' + r.subject || p.file !== f || !fs.existsSync(f) || p.sha256 !== sha(fs.readFileSync(f))) { bad.push(r.id + ': the preview is not its 0024 record'); continue }
    const t = (pm.trials || {})[p.from_trial] || {}
    if (t.task !== '0024' || typeof t.cost_usd !== 'number' || t.sha256 !== p.sha256 || !t.batch) bad.push(r.id + ': no matching paid 0024 trial')
    let prompt = null
    try { prompt = cs.getCartoonStyle(r.id).prompt } catch (e) {}
    if (!prompt || t.prompt_sha256 !== sha(Buffer.from(prompt))) bad.push(r.id + ': the trial was not rendered from the code prompt')
    const src = (sp.PREVIEW_SOURCES || {})[r.subject]
    if (!src || t.input_sha256 !== sha(fs.readFileSync(src.path))) bad.push(r.id + ': not rendered from the approved source of ' + r.subject)
    const w = (wm.files || {})[r.id] || {}
    if (w.from_sha256 !== p.sha256) bad.push(r.id + ': the web copy is not made from the preview')
  }
  const recs = [...Object.values(pm.trials || {}), ...(pm.discarded || [])].filter((x) => x && x.task === '0024' && x.batch)
  const spend = (n) => recs.filter((x) => n === 0 || x.batch === n).reduce((s, x) => s + (typeof x.cost_usd === 'number' ? x.cost_usd : 99), 0)
  if (spend(0) > 0.8) bad.push('0024 spent ' + spend(0).toFixed(4) + ' USD, over 0.80')
  for (const n of [1, 2]) if (spend(n) > 0.4) bad.push('batch ' + n + ' spent ' + spend(n).toFixed(4) + ' USD, over 0.40')
  const b = pm.batches_0024 || {}
  if (Object.keys(b).sort().join() !== '1,2' || Object.values(b).some((x) => !x.accepted_at)) bad.push('batches_0024 is not two accepted batches')
  return '100 old previews untouched; 20 new from accepted 0024 trials; spend ' + spend(0).toFixed(3) + ' USD'
}

C['126'] = async (bad) => {
  const h = await impHead('lib/cartoon-styles.ts', 'cs.ts')
  const cs = await imp('lib/cartoon-styles.ts')
  const se = await imp('lib/i18n/styles.en.ts')
  const sp = await imp('lib/style-previews.ts')
  const K = (s) => [s.id, s.name, s.description, s.category, s.closing, s.prompt]
  const S = cs.CARTOON_STYLES
  if (!eq(S.slice(0, h.CARTOON_STYLES.length).map(K), h.CARTOON_STYLES.map(K))) bad.push('the first ' + h.CARTOON_STYLES.length + ' styles are not HEAD\'s')
  const rows = styleRows()
  const added = S.slice(h.CARTOON_STYLES.length)
  if (added.map((s) => s.id).join() !== rows.map((r) => r.id).join()) bad.push('the added styles are [' + added.map((s) => s.id).join(', ') + ']')
  for (const r of rows) {
    const s = S.find((x) => x.id === r.id)
    if (!s) continue
    if (s.name !== r.trn || s.description !== r.trd || s.category !== r.category || s.closing !== 'preserve') bad.push(r.id + ': the Turkish record differs from §5.2')
    if (s.prompt !== r.body + ' ' + cs.CLOSING.preserve) bad.push(r.id + ': the prompt is not §5.2\'s body and the closing')
    const e = se.STYLE_TEXT_EN[r.id] || {}
    if (e.name !== r.enn || e.description !== r.end) bad.push(r.id + ': the English text differs from §5.2')
    if (sp.PREVIEW_SUBJECTS[r.id] !== r.subject) bad.push(r.id + ': subject ' + sp.PREVIEW_SUBJECTS[r.id] + ', not ' + r.subject)
  }
  if (!eq([...cs.STYLE_CATEGORIES], CATS)) bad.push('STYLE_CATEGORIES is ' + JSON.stringify(cs.STYLE_CATEGORIES))
  if (sp.STYLE_PREVIEW_IDS.join() !== S.map((s) => s.id).join()) bad.push('STYLE_PREVIEW_IDS is not the 120 ids in style order')
  return '100 untouched, then the 20 of §5.2'
}

C['133'] = async (bad) => {
  const NAMES = ['delete_account keeps the welcome e-mail hash', 'delete_account anonymises polar orders and checkouts', 'account delete refuses without the DELETE confirmation', 'account delete keeps the sign-in of a Firma Bulucu member', 'account overview returns no order id and no amount', 'session route answers signedIn false without a session']
  const files = fs.existsSync('tests') ? fs.readdirSync('tests') : []
  for (const f of files) { const s = fs.readFileSync('tests/' + f, 'utf8'); if (/supabase[.]co\b|eyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}|sb_secret_/.test(s)) bad.push('tests/' + f + ' names a real Supabase host or holds a key') }
  const r = spawnSync('npm', ['test'], { encoding: 'utf8', shell: true, timeout: 280000 })
  const out = String(r.stdout) + String(r.stderr)
  if (r.status !== 0) bad.push('npm test exited ' + r.status)
  const pass = /(pass|tests passed:?) +([0-9]+)/i.exec(out)
  const fail = /fail +([0-9]+)/i.exec(out)
  if (!pass || Number(pass[2]) < 36) bad.push('npm test reports ' + (pass ? pass[2] : 'no') + ' passing test(s), expected at least 36')
  if (!fail || fail[1] !== '0') bad.push('npm test reports failures')
  for (const n of NAMES) if (out.indexOf(n) < 0) bad.push('no test named: ' + n)
  return 'the 6 new required cases'
}

C['138'] = async (bad) => {
  const out = spawnSync('git', ['status', '--porcelain', '--untracked-files=all', '--', ':!.mavci'], { encoding: 'utf8' }).stdout || ''
  const allowed = ROUTES24.concat(PAGES24, ['components/site-header.tsx', 'components/profile-link.tsx', 'lib/supabase/server.ts', 'lib/account.ts', 'lib/i18n/paths.ts', 'lib/i18n/tr.ts', 'lib/i18n/en.ts', 'lib/i18n/styles.en.ts', 'lib/cartoon-styles.ts', 'lib/style-previews.ts', 'lib/preview-manifest.json', 'lib/style-web-manifest.json', 'app/globals.css', 'app/(tr)/(legal)/kvkk/page.tsx', 'app/(tr)/(legal)/privacy/page.tsx', MIG24, LIVE24, DOC24, 'scripts/check-styles.mjs', 'data/render-jobs-0024.json', 'reports/0024-choices.json', 'reports/0021-transfer.json', 'CHANGELOG.md', 'request-0024.txt'])
  const ids = styleRows().map((r) => r.id)
  const ok = (p) => allowed.includes(p) || /^tests[/][a-z0-9-]+([.]test)?[.](ts|mjs)$/.test(p) || /^components[/]account-[a-z-]+[.]tsx$/.test(p) || /^assets[/]trials[/]0024[/][a-z0-9-]+[.]A[.][A-Z][.]webp$/.test(p) || ids.some((id) => p === 'public/styles/' + id + '.webp' || p === 'public/styles-web/' + id + '.webp')
  let n = 0
  for (const line of out.split(LF).map((x) => (x.endsWith('\r') ? x.slice(0, -1) : x)).filter((x) => x.length > 3)) {
    n++
    let p = line.slice(3).trim()
    if (p.indexOf(' -> ') >= 0) p = p.split(' -> ')[1]
    if (p[0] === '"') p = JSON.parse(p)
    if (!ok(p)) bad.push(p + ' is outside task 0024 scope')
    else if (fs.existsSync(p) && !/[.](webp|jpg|png)$/.test(p)) {
      const b = fs.readFileSync(p)
      if (b[0] === 239 && b[1] === 187 && b[2] === 191) bad.push(p + ' has a BOM')
      if (b.toString('utf8').indexOf(String.fromCharCode(65533)) >= 0) bad.push(p + ' has U+FFFD')
    }
  }
  return n + ' changed path(s), all in scope'
}


/* ----------------------------------------------------- routes against stubs */

async function hit(ctx, method, p, jar, body) {
  const c0 = ctx.S.calls.length
  const h = {}
  if (jar) h.cookie = jar.header()
  if (body !== undefined) h['content-type'] = 'application/json'
  const r = await fetch(ctx.base + p, { method, headers: h, body: body === undefined ? undefined : typeof body === 'string' ? body : JSON.stringify(body) })
  if (jar) jar.take(r)
  let j = {}
  try { j = await r.json() } catch (e) {}
  return { status: r.status, body: j, cc: r.headers.get('cache-control') || '', cookies: r.headers.getSetCookie(), calls: ctx.S.calls.slice(c0) }
}
const rpcOf = (r, fn) => r.calls.filter((c) => c.k === 'rpc:' + fn)
const supa = (r) => r.calls.filter((c) => c.k.indexOf('rpc:') === 0 || c.k === 'user' || c.k.indexOf('admin:') === 0)

C['121'] = async (bad) => {
  const ctx = await boot({ port: 4241, stubPort: 4242 })
  try {
    let r = await hit(ctx, 'GET', '/api/session')
    if (r.status !== 200 || !eq(r.body, { signedIn: false }) || supa(r).length) bad.push('signed out: ' + r.status + ' ' + JSON.stringify(r.body) + ', ' + supa(r).length + ' Supabase call(s)')
    if (!/no-store/.test(r.cc)) bad.push('the session answer is cacheable')
    const u = await signIn(ctx, 'reader@example.com')
    r = await hit(ctx, 'GET', '/api/session', u.jar)
    if (r.status !== 200 || !eq(r.body, { signedIn: true })) bad.push('signed in: ' + r.status + ' ' + JSON.stringify(r.body))
    if (r.calls.some((c) => c.k.indexOf('rpc:') === 0)) bad.push('the session route called the database')
    ctx.S.userDown = true
    r = await hit(ctx, 'GET', '/api/session', u.jar)
    if (r.status !== 200 || !eq(r.body, { signedIn: false })) bad.push('Auth down: ' + r.status + ' ' + JSON.stringify(r.body))
    ctx.S.userDown = false
    r = await hit(ctx, 'POST', '/api/session', u.jar, {})
    if (r.status >= 200 && r.status < 300) bad.push('POST /api/session answered ' + r.status)
    return 'false without a session and without a call, true signed in, false when Auth is down'
  } finally { await ctx.close() }
}

C['122'] = async (bad) => {
  const ctx = await boot({ port: 4243, stubPort: 4244 })
  try {
    let r = await hit(ctx, 'GET', '/api/account')
    if (r.status !== 401 || r.body.code !== 'AUTH_REQUIRED' || r.calls.some((c) => c.k.indexOf('rpc:') === 0)) bad.push('signed out: ' + r.status + ' ' + r.body.code)
    const u = await signIn(ctx, 'reader@example.com')
    ctx.S.overview = { free_credits: 1, paid_credits: 35, purchases: [{ at: '2026-10-27T10:00:00Z', pack: 'p30', credits: 30, refund: 'partial' }], conversions: [{ at: '2026-10-27T11:00:00Z', style: 'classic', kind: 'free' }] }
    r = await hit(ctx, 'GET', '/api/account?user_id=' + UID(999), u.jar)
    const c = rpcOf(r, 'account_overview')
    if (r.status !== 200 || c.length !== 1 || c[0].args.p_user_id !== u.id) bad.push('signed in: ' + r.status + ', calls ' + JSON.stringify(c.map((x) => x.args)))
    if (Object.keys(r.body).sort().join() !== 'conversions,email,free,ok,paid,purchases') bad.push('the answer keys are ' + Object.keys(r.body).sort().join())
    if (r.body.ok !== true || r.body.email !== 'reader@example.com' || r.body.free !== 1 || r.body.paid !== 35 || !eq(r.body.purchases, ctx.S.overview.purchases) || !eq(r.body.conversions, ctx.S.overview.conversions)) bad.push('the answer is ' + JSON.stringify(r.body).slice(0, 200))
    if (!/no-store/.test(r.cc)) bad.push('the account answer is cacheable')
    ctx.S.rpcFail = 'account_overview'
    r = await hit(ctx, 'GET', '/api/account', u.jar)
    if (r.status !== 503 || /stub failure/.test(JSON.stringify(r.body))) bad.push('database down: ' + r.status + ' ' + JSON.stringify(r.body))
    ctx.S.rpcFail = null
    r = await hit(ctx, 'POST', '/api/account', u.jar, {})
    if (r.status >= 200 && r.status < 300) bad.push('POST /api/account answered ' + r.status)
    return '401, the session user only, exact keys, 503 without the error'
  } finally { await ctx.close() }
}

C['124'] = async (bad) => {
  const TR = await dict('tr')
  const ctx = await boot({ port: 4245, stubPort: 4246 })
  try {
    const cleared = (r) => r.cookies.filter((x) => x.indexOf('sb-') === 0).length > 0 && r.cookies.filter((x) => x.indexOf('sb-') === 0).every((x) => /max-age=0|expires=thu, 01 jan 1970/i.test(x))
    let r = await hit(ctx, 'POST', '/api/account/delete', null, { confirm: 'DELETE' })
    if (r.status !== 401 || r.body.code !== 'AUTH_REQUIRED' || supa(r).some((c) => c.k !== 'user')) bad.push('signed out: ' + r.status + ' ' + r.body.code)
    let u = await signIn(ctx, 'leaver@example.com')
    for (const [b, what] of [[undefined, 'no body'], ['not json', 'a non-JSON body'], [{}, 'an empty object'], [{ confirm: true }, 'confirm true'], [{ confirm: 'delete' }, 'lower-case delete']]) {
      r = await hit(ctx, 'POST', '/api/account/delete', u.jar, b)
      if (r.status !== 400 || r.body.code !== 'CONFIRM_REQUIRED' || r.body.message !== TR.errors.CONFIRM_REQUIRED || r.calls.some((c) => c.k.indexOf('rpc:') === 0 || c.k.indexOf('admin:') === 0)) bad.push(what + ': ' + r.status + ' ' + r.body.code)
    }
    ctx.S.del = { outcome: 'busy', keep_auth_user: true }
    r = await hit(ctx, 'POST', '/api/account/delete', u.jar, { confirm: 'DELETE' })
    if (r.status !== 409 || r.body.code !== 'CONVERSION_IN_PROGRESS' || r.calls.some((c) => c.k.indexOf('admin:') === 0)) bad.push('busy: ' + r.status + ' ' + r.body.code)
    ctx.S.rpcFail = 'delete_account'
    ctx.S.del = null
    r = await hit(ctx, 'POST', '/api/account/delete', u.jar, { confirm: 'DELETE' })
    if (r.status !== 503 || r.calls.some((c) => c.k.indexOf('admin:') === 0) || /stub failure/.test(JSON.stringify(r.body))) bad.push('the RPC failing: ' + r.status + ', ' + r.calls.map((c) => c.k).join())
    ctx.S.rpcFail = null
    r = await hit(ctx, 'POST', '/api/account/delete', u.jar, { confirm: 'DELETE', user_id: UID(999) })
    const ks = r.calls.map((c) => c.k)
    const d = rpcOf(r, 'delete_account')
    const ad = r.calls.filter((c) => c.k === 'admin:DELETE')
    if (r.status !== 200 || !eq(r.body, { ok: true, auth: 'deleted' })) bad.push('a deletion answered ' + r.status + ' ' + JSON.stringify(r.body))
    if (d.length !== 1 || d[0].args.p_user_id !== u.id) bad.push('delete_account got ' + JSON.stringify(d.map((x) => x.args)))
    if (ad.length !== 1 || ad[0].id !== u.id || ad[0].key !== SERVICE) bad.push('the admin delete was ' + JSON.stringify(ad))
    if (ks.indexOf('rpc:delete_account') < 0 || ks.indexOf('admin:DELETE') < ks.indexOf('rpc:delete_account')) bad.push('the auth user was not deleted after the records: ' + ks.join())
    if (!cleared(r)) bad.push('the session cookies were not cleared')
    u = await signIn(ctx, 'member@example.com')
    ctx.S.del = { outcome: 'deleted', keep_auth_user: true }
    r = await hit(ctx, 'POST', '/api/account/delete', u.jar, { confirm: 'DELETE' })
    if (r.status !== 200 || !eq(r.body, { ok: true, auth: 'kept_member' }) || r.calls.some((c) => c.k.indexOf('admin:') === 0) || !cleared(r)) bad.push('a member: ' + r.status + ' ' + JSON.stringify(r.body) + ' ' + r.calls.map((c) => c.k).join())
    u = await signIn(ctx, 'blocked@example.com')
    ctx.S.del = null
    ctx.S.adminFail = true
    r = await hit(ctx, 'POST', '/api/account/delete', u.jar, { confirm: 'DELETE' })
    if (r.status !== 200 || !eq(r.body, { ok: true, auth: 'failed' }) || !cleared(r)) bad.push('a blocked auth delete: ' + r.status + ' ' + JSON.stringify(r.body))
    ctx.S.adminFail = false
    r = await hit(ctx, 'GET', '/api/account/delete', u.jar)
    if (r.status >= 200 && r.status < 300) bad.push('GET /api/account/delete answered ' + r.status)
    return 'confirmation, busy, records then auth user with the service key, member kept, failure told, cookies cleared'
  } finally { await ctx.close() }
}


/* ------------------------------------------------------------- browser */

async function browser(ctx, dbg, handler) {
  const exe = ['C:/Program Files/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe', 'C:/Program Files (x86)/Google/Chrome/Application/chrome.exe', 'C:/Program Files (x86)/Microsoft/Edge/Application/msedge.exe'].find((p) => fs.existsSync(p))
  if (!exe) throw new Error('no Chrome or Edge found')
  const kid = spawn(exe, ['--headless=new', '--disable-gpu', '--no-first-run', '--user-data-dir=' + fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'cdp-')), '--remote-debugging-port=' + dbg, 'about:blank'], { stdio: 'ignore' })
  const t = await until(async () => (await (await fetch('http://127.0.0.1:' + dbg + '/json/list')).json()).find((x) => x.type === 'page'), 30000, 'chrome')
  const ws = new WebSocket(t.webSocketDebuggerUrl)
  await new Promise((r, j) => { ws.onopen = r; ws.onerror = j })
  let seq = 0
  const pend = new Map()
  const send = (method, params) => new Promise((r, j) => { const id = ++seq; pend.set(id, { r, j }); ws.send(JSON.stringify({ id, method, params: params || {} })) })
  const reply = (id, status, body) => send('Fetch.fulfillRequest', { requestId: id, responseCode: status, responseHeaders: [{ name: 'content-type', value: 'application/json' }], body: Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(body)).toString('base64') })
  ws.onmessage = (m) => {
    const d = JSON.parse(m.data)
    if (d.id && pend.has(d.id)) { const p = pend.get(d.id); pend.delete(d.id); if (d.error) p.j(new Error(d.error.message)); else p.r(d.result); return }
    if (d.method !== 'Fetch.requestPaused') return
    const rq = d.params.request
    const a = handler(new URL(rq.url).pathname, rq)
    if (a) return reply(d.params.requestId, a[0], a[1])
    send('Fetch.continueRequest', { requestId: d.params.requestId })
  }
  await send('Fetch.enable', { patterns: [{ urlPattern: '*/api/*' }] })
  await send('Page.enable')
  await send('Runtime.enable')
  const ev = async (e) => (await send('Runtime.evaluate', { expression: typeof e === 'function' ? '(' + e + ')()' : e, returnByValue: true, awaitPromise: true })).result.value
  const size = (w, h) => send('Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride', { width: w, height: h, deviceScaleFactor: 1, mobile: w < 600 })
  const go = async (u) => { await send('Page.navigate', { url: ctx.base + u }); await until(() => ev(() => document.readyState === 'complete'), 30000, u); await sleep(600) }
  const click = (sel) => ev('(()=>{const e=document.querySelector(' + JSON.stringify(sel) + ');if(!e)return false;e.click();return true})()')
  const vis = (sel) => ev('(()=>{const e=document.querySelector(' + JSON.stringify(sel) + ');if(!e)return false;const r=e.getBoundingClientRect();return r.width>0&&r.height>0})()')
  const text = () => ev(() => document.body.innerText)
  return { send, ev, size, go, click, vis, text, close: () => { try { ws.close(); kid.kill() } catch (e) {} } }
}

C['131'] = async (bad) => {
  const TR = await dict('tr')
  const EN = await dict('en')
  const ctx = await boot({ port: 4247, stubPort: 4248 })
  const M = { session: false, account: [401, { ok: false, code: 'AUTH_REQUIRED', message: 'x' }], del: [200, { ok: true, auth: 'deleted' }], dels: [], signouts: 0, sessions: 0 }
  let b
  try {
    b = await browser(ctx, 4997, (p, rq) => {
      if (p === '/api/session') { M.sessions++; return [200, { signedIn: M.session }] }
      if (p === '/api/account') return M.account
      if (p === '/api/account/delete') { M.dels.push(rq.postData || ''); return M.del }
      if (p === '/api/auth/sign-out') { M.signouts++; return [200, { ok: true }] }
      if (p === '/api/wallet') return [200, { ok: true, email: 'r@example.com', free: 2, paid: 0, welcome: 'already' }]
      return null
    })
    await b.size(1280, 800)
    await b.go('/account')
    await until(() => b.vis('.account-signed-out'), 15000, 'the signed-out profile')
    if (!(await b.ev(() => !!document.querySelector('.account-signed-out a[href^="/workshop"]')))) bad.push('signed out: no link to the workshop')
    if (await b.vis('a.site-nav-profile')) bad.push('the header shows Profil while signed out')
    M.session = true
    M.account = [200, { ok: true, email: 'reader@example.com', free: 1, paid: 35, purchases: [{ at: '2026-10-27T10:00:00Z', pack: 'p75', credits: 75, refund: 'full' }, { at: '2026-10-26T10:00:00Z', pack: 'p30', credits: 30, refund: 'partial' }, { at: '2026-10-25T10:00:00Z', pack: 'p10', credits: 10, refund: 'none' }], conversions: [{ at: '2026-10-27T11:00:00Z', style: 'pet-oil-portrait', kind: 'paid' }, { at: '2026-10-24T11:00:00Z', style: 'classic', kind: 'free' }] }]
    await b.go('/account')
    await until(() => b.vis('.account-balance'), 15000, 'the balance')
    const bal = await b.ev(() => { const e = document.querySelector('.account-balance'); return [e.getAttribute('data-free'), e.getAttribute('data-paid')] })
    if (!eq(bal, ['1', '35'])) bad.push('the balance carries ' + JSON.stringify(bal))
    const pu = await b.ev(() => [...document.querySelectorAll('.account-purchases li')].map((l) => [l.getAttribute('data-pack'), l.getAttribute('data-refund'), l.innerText]))
    const wantP = [['p75', 'full', '$19.99', TR.profile.refundFull], ['p30', 'partial', '$9.99', TR.profile.refundPartial], ['p10', 'none', '$3.99', TR.profile.refundNone]]
    if (pu.length !== 3 || wantP.some((w, i) => !pu[i] || pu[i][0] !== w[0] || pu[i][1] !== w[1] || pu[i][2].indexOf(w[2]) < 0 || pu[i][2].indexOf(w[3]) < 0)) bad.push('the purchases are ' + JSON.stringify(pu))
    const cv = await b.ev(() => [...document.querySelectorAll('.account-conversions li')].map((l) => [l.getAttribute('data-style'), l.getAttribute('data-kind'), l.innerText, l.querySelectorAll('img,picture,canvas').length]))
    if (cv.length !== 2 || cv[0][0] !== 'pet-oil-portrait' || cv[0][1] !== 'paid' || cv[0][2].indexOf('Yağlı Boya Evcil Portre') < 0 || cv[0][2].indexOf(TR.profile.paid) < 0 || cv[1][2].indexOf('Klasik Karikatür') < 0 || cv[1][2].indexOf(TR.profile.free) < 0 || cv.some((x) => x[3])) bad.push('the conversions are ' + JSON.stringify(cv))
    const body = await b.text()
    for (const k of ['title', 'creditsTitle', 'purchasesTitle', 'conversionsTitle', 'conversionsNote', 'languageTitle', 'signOut', 'deleteButton']) if (body.indexOf(TR.profile[k]) < 0) bad.push('the profile does not show profile.' + k)
    const fs1 = await b.ev(() => [parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('main.account-page')).fontSize), parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.querySelector('.account-balance')).fontSize), Math.max(...[...document.querySelectorAll('.account-balance *')].map((e) => parseFloat(getComputedStyle(e).fontSize)))])
    if (!(fs1[0] >= 18) || !(Math.max(fs1[1], fs1[2]) >= 32)) bad.push('type sizes are ' + JSON.stringify(fs1))
    const langs = await b.ev(() => [...document.querySelectorAll('a.account-lang')].map((a) => [a.getAttribute('hreflang'), a.getAttribute('href')]))
    if (!eq(langs, [['tr', '/account'], ['en', '/en/account']])) bad.push('the language links are ' + JSON.stringify(langs))
    const prof = await b.ev(() => { const a = document.querySelector('.site-nav a.site-nav-profile'); return a ? [a.getAttribute('href'), a.innerText] : null })
    if (!prof || prof[0] !== '/account' || prof[1].indexOf(TR.header.profile) < 0) bad.push('the header profile link is ' + JSON.stringify(prof))
    await b.click('button.account-buy')
    await until(() => b.ev(() => document.querySelectorAll('.credit-pack').length === 3), 10000, 'the three packs')
    await b.click('button.account-delete')
    await until(() => b.vis('.account-delete-confirm'), 10000, 'the confirmation')
    if ((await b.text()).indexOf(TR.profile.deleteWarning) < 0) bad.push('the confirmation does not show the warning')
    if (M.dels.length) bad.push('the first click sent a request')
    await b.click('button.account-delete-cancel')
    await sleep(400)
    if (await b.vis('.account-delete-confirm')) bad.push('Cancel did not close the confirmation')
    for (const [auth, key] of [['deleted', 'deletedAll'], ['kept_member', 'deletedKept'], ['failed', 'deletedFailed']]) {
      M.dels = []
      M.del = [200, { ok: true, auth }]
      await b.go('/account')
      await until(() => b.vis('button.account-delete'), 15000, 'the delete button')
      await b.click('button.account-delete')
      await until(() => b.vis('button.account-delete-yes'), 10000, 'the yes button')
      await b.click('button.account-delete-yes')
      await until(() => b.vis('.account-deleted[data-auth="' + auth + '"]'), 10000, 'the ' + auth + ' result')
      if ((await b.text()).indexOf(TR.profile[key]) < 0) bad.push(auth + ' does not show profile.' + key)
      if (M.dels.length !== 1 || M.dels[0] !== '{"confirm":"DELETE"}') bad.push(auth + ': sent ' + JSON.stringify(M.dels))
    }
    M.signouts = 0
    await b.go('/account')
    await until(() => b.vis('button.account-signout'), 15000, 'sign-out')
    await b.click('button.account-signout')
    await until(() => M.signouts > 0, 10000, 'the sign-out request')
    await b.go('/en/account')
    await until(() => b.vis('.account-balance'), 15000, 'the English profile')
    const eb = await b.text()
    for (const k of ['title', 'purchasesTitle', 'conversionsTitle']) if (eb.indexOf(EN.profile[k]) < 0) bad.push('/en/account does not show ' + k)
    if (eb.indexOf('Oil Pet Portrait') < 0) bad.push('/en/account does not show the English style name')
    if ((await b.ev(() => (document.querySelector('a.site-nav-profile') || {}).getAttribute && document.querySelector('a.site-nav-profile').getAttribute('href'))) !== '/en/account') bad.push('the English header does not link /en/account')
    await b.size(390, 844)
    await b.go('/account')
    await until(() => b.vis('.account-balance'), 15000, 'the phone profile')
    if (await b.ev(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth > innerWidth)) bad.push('the profile overflows at 390 px')
    return 'signed out, balances, history without images, packs, confirmation, three results, sign-out, English, header link'
  } finally { if (b) b.close(); await ctx.close() }
}

C['132'] = async (bad) => {
  const css = (R('app/globals.css') || '').replace(/[/][*][^]*?[*][/]/g, '')
  const m = /(^|\n)\.style-filter\s*\{([^}]*)\}/.exec(css)
  if (!m || !/flex-wrap:\s*nowrap/.test(m[2]) || !/overflow-x:\s*auto/.test(m[2])) bad.push('.style-filter is not nowrap with overflow-x auto')
  const cs = await imp('lib/cartoon-styles.ts')
  const ctx = await boot({ port: 4249, stubPort: 4250 })
  let b
  try {
    b = await browser(ctx, 4998, (p) => (p === '/api/wallet' ? [200, { ok: true, email: 'r@example.com', free: 2, paid: 0, welcome: 'already' }] : p === '/api/session' ? [200, { signedIn: true }] : null))
    for (const [w, h] of [[390, 844], [1280, 800]]) {
      await b.size(w, h)
      await b.go('/workshop')
      await until(() => b.ev(() => !!document.querySelector('#cartoonify-image-input')), 15000, 'the upload input')
      const doc = await b.send('DOM.getDocument', { depth: -1 })
      const n = await b.send('DOM.querySelector', { nodeId: doc.root.nodeId, selector: '#cartoonify-image-input' })
      await b.send('DOM.setFileInputFiles', { nodeId: n.nodeId, files: [path.resolve('public/hero/before.jpg')] })
      await until(() => b.ev(() => !!document.querySelector('.style-filter')), 15000, 'the filter strip')
      await sleep(400)
      const st = await b.ev(() => { const f = document.querySelector('.style-filter'); const bs = [...f.querySelectorAll('button')]; return { cats: bs.map((x) => x.dataset.category), tops: [...new Set(bs.map((x) => Math.round(x.getBoundingClientRect().top + f.scrollTop)))].length, over: document.documentElement.scrollWidth > innerWidth, scroll: f.scrollWidth > f.clientWidth } })
      if (st.cats.join() !== ['all'].concat(CATS).join()) bad.push(w + ' px: the tabs are ' + st.cats.join())
      if (st.tops !== 1) bad.push(w + ' px: the tabs sit on ' + st.tops + ' lines')
      if (st.over) bad.push(w + ' px: the page is wider than the viewport')
      if (w === 390 && !st.scroll) bad.push('390 px: the strip does not scroll')
      for (const c of ['pet', 'avatar']) {
        await b.click('.style-filter button[data-category="' + c + '"]')
        await sleep(500)
        const vis = await b.ev(() => [...document.querySelectorAll('.style-card')].filter((e) => e.getClientRects().length > 0).map((e) => e.querySelector('input').value).sort().join())
        const want = cs.CARTOON_STYLES.filter((s) => s.category === c).map((s) => s.id).sort().join()
        if (vis !== want || want.split(',').length !== 10) bad.push(w + ' px, ' + c + ': shows ' + vis)
      }
    }
    return 'one row of 15 tabs at 390 and 1280 px, scrolls on the phone, 10 + 10 styles'
  } finally { if (b) b.close(); await ctx.close() }
}

criterion 4 says which'); if(!(lum(T['--bg'])>=0.8)) bad.push('--bg is not light: luminance '+lum(T['--bg']).toFixed(3)); if(!(lum(T['--text'])<=0.05)) bad.push('--text is not dark: luminance '+lum(T['--text']).toFixed(3)); rows.push(t+'/'+a+'='+r.toFixed(2)); if(r<4.5) bad.push(t+' on '+a+' is '+r.toFixed(2)+':1'); } if(bad.length) throw new Error(bad.join('; ')); console.log('ok: '+rows.length+' pairs, lowest '+rows.map(r=>parseFloat(r.split('=')[1])).sort((a,b)=>a-b)[0].toFixed(2)+':1')\""

if(css.indexOf(':root',ri+1)>=0) throw new Error('more than one :root block'); const rb=css.indexOf('{',ri), re=css.indexOf('}',rb); const rootBody=css.slice(rb+1,re); const rest=css.slice(0,ri)+css.slice(re+1); const T={}; for(const d of rootBody.split(';')){ const s=d.trim(); if(s.indexOf('--')!==0) continue; const k=s.slice(0,s.indexOf(':')).trim(); T[k]=s.slice(s.indexOf(':')+1).trim().toLowerCase(); } const HEX='0123456789abcdef'; const rgbOf=v=>{ if(typeof v!=='string'||v.charAt(0)!=='#'||!(v.length===4||v.length===7)) return null; let h=v.slice(1); if(!h.split('').every(c=>HEX.indexOf(c)>=0)) return null; if(h.length===3) h=h.split('').map(c=>c+c).join(''); if(i<0) continue; decls[d.slice(0,i).trim().toLowerCase()]=d.slice(i+1).trim(); } rules.push({sel:m[1].trim(), decls}); } const TEXT=['--text','--muted','--accent-text','--danger-text','--success-text'].filter(k=>k in T); const SURF=Object.keys(T).filter(k=>k==='--bg'||k.indexOf('--surface')===0); const bad=[]; const okColour=['inherit','currentcolor','transparent','var(--on-accent)'].concat(TEXT.map(k=>'var('+k+')')); const v=d[p].toLowerCase(); const vars=v.split('var(').slice(1).map(x=>x.slice(0,x.indexOf(')')).split(',')[0].trim()); for(const x of vars){ if(x==='--accent'||x==='--accent-2') accent=true; else if(SURF.indexOf(x)<0) bad.push(r.sel.slice(-40)+' { '+p+' } uses '+x+', not a checked surface'); } const words=v.replace(/var[(][^)]*[)]/g,' ').replace(/(linear|radial|conic)-gradient|transparent|ellipse|circle|bottom|right|left|none|top|deg|to|at|%|[0-9.,()-]/g,' ').trim(); if(words) bad.push(r.sel.slice(-40)+' { '+p+' } has '+words.slice(0,30)); } if(accent&&'color' in d&&['var(--on-accent)','var(--text)'].indexOf(d.color.toLowerCase())<0) bad.push(r.sel.slice(-40)+' has an accent background with color '+d.color); } if(bad.length) throw new Error(bad.length+' problem(s): '+bad.slice(0,8).join('; ')); console.log('ok: '+rules.length+' rules')\""

const wantF=want.map(r=>'public/gallery/'+r[0]+'/source.webp'); if(files.slice().sort().join()!==wantF.slice().sort().join()) bad.push('public/gallery holds '+files.join()); let total=0; for(const f of wantF){ if(!fs.existsSync(f)) continue; const b=fs.readFileSync(f); total+=b.length; const d=dims(b); if(!d||d[0]!==480||d[1]!==480) bad.push(f+' is '+(d?d.join('x'):'not WebP')+', not 480x480'); if(b.length>100000) bad.push(f+' is '+b.length+' bytes'); } if(bad.length) throw new Error(bad.length+' problem(s): '+bad.slice(0,8).join('; ')); console.log('ok: 5 rows equal to the spec, 15 distinct styles each on its row subject, 5 source copies at 480 ('+total+' bytes), alts in tr and en'); i=s.indexOf('>', j); } for(const at of ['alt','aria-label','title','placeholder']){ let p=s.indexOf(at+'='+Q); while(p>=0){ const v=s.slice(p+at.length+2, s.indexOf(Q, p+at.length+2)); if(letter.test(v)) bad.push(f+': '+at+' '+JSON.stringify(v.slice(0,40))); p=s.indexOf(at+'='+Q, p+1); } } const parts=s.split(A); for(let k=1;k<parts.length;k+=2){ const v=parts[k]; if(v.indexOf(' ')>=0 && letter.test(v) && v!=='use client' && !/Error[(]$/.test(parts[k-1]) && !v.trim().split(/ +/).every(cls)) bad.push(f+': string '+JSON.stringify(v.slice(0,40)));     "run": "node -e \"const fs=require('fs'); const css=fs.readFileSync('app/globals.css','utf8').replace(/[/][*][^]*?[*][/]/g,''); const rules=[]; const rx=/([^{}]+)[{]([^{}]*)[}]/g; let m; while((m=rx.exec(css))){ const d={}; for(const x of m[2].split(';')){ const i=x.indexOf(':'); if(i<0) continue; d[x.slice(0,i).trim().toLowerCase()]=x.slice(i+1).trim(); } rules.push({sel:m[1].trim().split(String.fromCharCode(10)).map(s=>s.trim()).filter(Boolean).join(' '), d, at:m.index}); } const media=css.split('@media').slice(1).map(c=>({q:c.slice(0,c.indexOf('{')).trim(), body:c})); const bad=[]; const root=rules.find(r=>r.sel===':root'); if(!root) throw new Error('no :root rule'); if(root.d['--page-max']!=='1440px') bad.push('--page-max is '+root.d['--page-max']+', not 1440px'); for(const s of ['main','footer','.site-header']){ const r=rules.find(x=>x.sel===s&&'width' in x.d); if(!r) { bad.push('no top-level '+s+' width rule'); continue; } if(r.d.width.replace(/ /g,'')!=='min(var(--page-max),100%)') bad.push(s+' width is '+r.d.width); } if(css.indexOf('1200px')>=0) bad.push('a 1200px width is left in the stylesheet'); if(bad.length) throw new Error(bad.join('; ')); console.log('ok: main, footer, header at min(var(--page-max), 100%), --page-max 1440px')\""

    "run": "node -e \"const fs=require('fs'); const css=fs.readFileSync('app/globals.css','utf8').replace(/[/][*][^]*?[*][/]/g,''); const rules=[]; const rx=/([^{}]+)[{]([^{}]*)[}]/g; let m; while((m=rx.exec(css))){ const d={}; for(const x of m[2].split(';')){ const i=x.indexOf(':'); if(i<0) continue; d[x.slice(0,i).trim().toLowerCase()]=x.slice(i+1).trim(); } rules.push({sel:m[1].trim().split(String.fromCharCode(10)).map(s=>s.trim()).filter(Boolean).join(' '), d, at:m.index}); } const media=css.split('@media').slice(1).map(c=>({q:c.slice(0,c.indexOf('{')).trim(), body:c.slice(c.indexOf('{')+1)})); const inMedia=(test,sel)=>{ const out={}; for(const mm of media){ if(!test(mm.q)) continue; const r2=/([^{}]+)[{]([^{}]*)[}]/g; let x; while((x=r2.exec(mm.body))){ if(x[1].trim()!==sel) continue; for(const y of x[2].split(';')){ const i=y.indexOf(':'); if(i>0) out[y.slice(0,i).trim()]=y.slice(i+1).trim(); } } } return out; }; const wide=q=>/min-width/.test(q); const narrow=q=>/max-width: *(899|899[.][0-9]+)px/.test(q); const nows=s=>String(s||'').replace(/ /g,''); const bad=[]; const root=rules.find(r=>r.sel===':root'); const pw=root&&root.d['--panel-w']; const n=parseFloat(pw); if(!pw||!String(pw).endsWith('px')||!(n>=320&&n<=380)) bad.push('--panel-w is '+pw+', not 320px..380px'); const sh=inMedia(wide,'.workshop-shell'); if(nows(sh['grid-template-columns'])!=='var(--panel-w)minmax(0,1fr)') bad.push('wide .workshop-shell columns are '+sh['grid-template-columns']); const g=inMedia(wide,'.style-grid'); if(nows(g['grid-template-columns']).indexOf('repeat(auto-fill,minmax(190px,1fr))')!==0) bad.push('wide .style-grid is '+g['grid-template-columns']+', not auto-fill at a 190px floor'); if(bad.length) throw new Error(bad.join('; ')); console.log('ok: panel '+pw+', cards auto-fill at 190px')\""
